1→"""plan command: dispatcher for plan subcommands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     8→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan, save_plan
     9→
    10→
    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    12→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    13→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan_cmd import cmd_plan_output
    14→
    15→    cmd_plan_output(args)
    16→
    17→
    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    24→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→
    36→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    37→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    38→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    39→    if total_skipped:
    40→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    41→    else:
    42→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    43→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    44→    if clusters:
    45→        manual = {n: c for n, c in clusters.items() if not c.get("auto")}
    46→        auto = {n: c for n, c in clusters.items() if c.get("auto")}
    47→        if manual:
    48→            for name, cluster in manual.items():
    49→                desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    50→                member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    51→                marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    52→                desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    53→                print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    54→        if auto:
    55→            auto_items = sum(len(c.get("finding_ids", [])) for c in auto.values())
    56→            focused_auto = f" (focused: {active})" if active and active in auto else ""
    57→            print(f"    ({len(auto)} auto-clusters, {auto_items} findings{focused_auto})")
    58→    if described or noted:
    59→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    60→    if active:
    61→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    62→    if superseded:
    63→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    64→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
    65→    if epics:
    66→        print(f"  Epics:            {len(epics)}")
    67→        for name, epic in epics.items():
    68→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
    69→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
    70→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
    71→            fcount = len(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
    72→            safe_tag = " [agent-safe]" if epic.get("agent_safe") else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name} [{direction}] ({fcount} findings, {status}){safe_tag}")
    74→            if thesis:
    75→                print(f"      {thesis}")
    76→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    77→    if meta and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
    78→        print(f"  Strategy:         {meta['strategy_summary']}")
    79→        print(f"  Last synthesis:   v{meta.get('version', 0)} ({meta.get('trigger', 'unknown')} @ {meta.get('last_run', 'never')})")
    80→
    81→
    82→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    83→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    84→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    85→
    86→    plan = load_plan()
    87→    reset_plan(plan)
    88→    save_plan(plan)
    89→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    90→
    91→
    92→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    93→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    94→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    95→
    96→    if plan_action is None:
    97→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if plan_action == "show":
   101→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "queue":
   105→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
   106→
   107→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   108→        return
   109→
   110→    if plan_action == "reset":
   111→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   112→        return
   113→
   114→    if plan_action == "move":
   115→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
   116→
   117→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    if plan_action in (
   121→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   122→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   123→    ):
   124→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
   125→            cmd_plan_describe,
   126→            cmd_plan_done,
   127→            cmd_plan_focus,
   128→            cmd_plan_note,
   129→            cmd_plan_reopen,
   130→            cmd_plan_skip,
   131→            cmd_plan_unskip,
   132→        )
   133→
   134→        dispatch = {
   135→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   136→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   137→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   138→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   139→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   140→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   141→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   142→        }
   143→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   147→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   148→
   149→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   153→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   154→
   155→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   159→
   160→
   161→# Backwards-compatible alias
   162→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   163→
   164→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   165→

1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_subjective import (
     7→    build_subjective_followup,
     8→)
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    10→    scorecard_subjective_entries,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE, group_queue_items
    13→from desloppify.intelligence.integrity import (
    14→    is_holistic_subjective_finding,
    15→    subjective_review_open_breakdown,
    16→    unassessed_subjective_dimensions,
    17→)
    18→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    19→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    20→
    21→
    22→def scorecard_subjective(
    23→    state: dict,
    24→    dim_scores: dict,
    25→) -> list[dict]:
    26→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    27→    if not dim_scores:
    28→        return []
    29→    return scorecard_subjective_entries(
    30→        state,
    31→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
    32→    )
    33→
    34→
    35→def subjective_coverage_breakdown(
    36→    findings_scoped: dict,
    37→) -> tuple[int, dict[str, int], dict[str, int]]:
    38→    """Return open subjective-review count plus reason and holistic-reason breakdowns."""
    39→    return subjective_review_open_breakdown(findings_scoped)
    40→
    41→
    42→def _tier_label(tier: int) -> str:
    43→    return f"T{tier}"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _render_tier_navigator(queue: dict) -> None:
    47→    counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    48→    print(colorize("\n  Tier Navigator", "bold"))
    49→    print(
    50→        colorize(
    51→            f"    Open: T1:{counts.get(1, 0)} T2:{counts.get(2, 0)} "
    52→            f"T3:{counts.get(3, 0)} T4:{counts.get(4, 0)}",
    53→            "dim",
    54→        )
    55→    )
    56→    print(
    57→        colorize(
    58→            "    Switch: `desloppify next --tier 1` | `desloppify next --tier 2` | "
    59→            "`desloppify next --tier 3` | `desloppify next --tier 4`",
    60→            "dim",
    61→        )
    62→    )
    63→
    64→
    65→def _render_grouped(items: list[dict], group: str) -> None:
    66→    grouped = group_queue_items(items, group)
    67→    for key, grouped_items in grouped.items():
    68→        print(colorize(f"\n  {key} ({len(grouped_items)})", "cyan"))
    69→        for item in grouped_items:
    70→            tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
    71→            tag = _effort_tag(item)
    72→            tag_str = f" {tag}" if tag else ""
    73→            print(
    74→                f"    {_tier_label(tier)} [{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]{tag_str} {item.get('summary', '')}"
    75→            )
    76→
    77→
    78→def is_auto_fix_command(command: str | None) -> bool:
    79→    cmd = (command or "").strip()
    80→    return cmd.startswith("desloppify fix ") and "--dry-run" in cmd
    81→
    82→
    83→def _effort_tag(item: dict) -> str:
    84→    """Return a short effort/type tag for a queue item."""
    85→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    86→        return "[review]"
    87→    if is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    88→        return "[auto]"
    89→    return ""
    90→
    91→
    92→_ACTION_TYPE_LABELS = {
    93→    "auto_fix": "Auto-fixable batch",
    94→    "reorganize": "Reorganize batch",
    95→    "refactor": "Refactor batch",
    96→    "manual_fix": "Grouped task",
    97→}
    98→
    99→
   100→def _render_cluster_item(item: dict) -> None:
   101→    """Render an auto-cluster task card."""
   102→    member_count = int(item.get("member_count", 0))
   103→    action_type = item.get("action_type", "manual_fix")
   104→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   105→    if cluster_name == "auto/initial-review":
   106→        type_label = "Initial subjective review"
   107→    elif cluster_name == "auto/stale-review":
   108→        type_label = "Stale subjective review"
   109→    else:
   110→        type_label = _ACTION_TYPE_LABELS.get(action_type, "Grouped task")
   111→    print(colorize(f"  ({type_label}, {member_count} findings)", "bold"))
   112→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   113→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   114→
   115→    # Show breakdown by file (compact) + representative members
   116→    members = item.get("members", [])
   117→    if members:
   118→        # File distribution
   119→        from collections import Counter
   120→        file_counts = Counter(m.get("file", "?") for m in members)
   121→        if len(file_counts) <= 5:
   122→            print(colorize("\n  Files:", "dim"))
   123→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common():
   124→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   125→        else:
   126→            print(colorize(f"\n  Spread across {len(file_counts)} files:", "dim"))
   127→            for f, c in file_counts.most_common(3):
   128→                print(f"    {f} ({c})")
   129→            remaining = len(file_counts) - 3
   130→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {remaining} more files", "dim"))
   131→
   132→        # Sample IDs
   133→        print(colorize("\n  Sample:", "dim"))
   134→        for m in members[:3]:
   135→            print(f"    - {m.get('id', '')}")
   136→        if len(members) > 3:
   137→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(members) - 3} more", "dim"))
   138→
   139→    # Action
   140→    cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   141→    primary_command = item.get("primary_command")
   142→    if primary_command:
   143→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {primary_command}", "cyan"))
   144→
   145→    # Resolution hints
   146→    print(colorize(f'\n  Resolve all:   desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" '
   147→                   f'--note "<what>" --attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"', "dim"))
   148→    print(colorize(f"  Drill in:      desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10",
   149→                   "dim"))
   150→    print(colorize(f"  Skip cluster:  desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   151→
   152→
   153→def _render_item(
   154→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   155→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   156→) -> None:
   157→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
   158→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   159→        _render_cluster_item(item)
   160→        return
   161→
   162→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   163→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   164→    print(colorize(f"  (Tier {tier}, {confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
   165→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   166→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
   167→
   168→    # Effort/type label
   169→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
   170→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
   171→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
   172→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
   173→
   174→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   175→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
   176→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
   177→        subjective_score = float(
   178→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
   179→        )
   180→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
   181→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")
   182→        print(
   183→            colorize(
   184→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
   185→                "cyan",
   186→            )
   187→        )
   188→        print(colorize(
   189→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
   190→            "dim",
   191→        ))
   192→        if explain:
   193→            reason = item.get("explain", {}).get(
   194→                "policy",
   195→                "subjective items are fixed at T4 and do not outrank mechanical T1/T2/T3.",
   196→            )
   197→            print(colorize(f"  explain: {reason}", "dim"))
   198→        return
   199→
   200→    # Plan overrides: description, cluster, note
   201→    if item.get("plan_description"):
   202→        print(colorize(f"  → {item['plan_description']}", "cyan"))
   203→    plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   204→    if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict):
   205→        cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "")
   206→        cluster_desc = plan_cluster.get("description") or ""
   207→        total = plan_cluster.get("total_items", 0)
   208→        desc_str = f' — "{cluster_desc}"' if cluster_desc else ""
   209→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}{desc_str} ({total} items)", "dim"))
   210→    if item.get("plan_note"):
   211→        print(colorize(f"  Note: {item['plan_note']}", "dim"))
   212→
   213→    file = item.get("file", "")
   214→    if file and file != ".":
   215→        print(f"  File: {file}")
   216→    print(colorize(f"  ID:   {item.get('id', '')}", "dim"))
   217→
   218→    detail = item.get("detail", {})
   219→    if detail.get("lines"):
   220→        print(f"  Lines: {', '.join(str(line_no) for line_no in detail['lines'][:8])}")
   221→    if detail.get("category"):
   222→        print(f"  Category: {detail['category']}")
   223→    if detail.get("importers") is not None:
   224→        print(f"  Active importers: {detail['importers']}")
   225→    if detail.get("suggestion"):
   226→        print(colorize(f"\n  Suggestion: {detail['suggestion']}", "dim"))
   227→
   228→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   229→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   230→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   231→        if snippet:
   232→            print(colorize("\n  Code:", "dim"))
   233→            print(snippet)
   234→
   235→    if dim_scores:
   236→        detector = item.get("detector", "")
   237→        dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   238→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   239→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   240→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   241→            print(

   246→                    "dim",

   248→            )
   249→
   250→    # Score impact estimate

   255→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   256→            if impact > 0:
   257→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   258→            else:
   259→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   260→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   261→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   262→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   263→                    if issues > 1:
   264→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   265→                        if bulk > 0:

   268→                                "cyan",
   269→                            ))
   270→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   271→            pass
   272→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   273→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   274→        try:
   275→            from desloppify.scoring import compute_health_breakdown
   276→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   277→            if dim_key:

   289→                            ))
   290→                        break
   291→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   292→            pass
   293→
   294→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   295→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   296→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):

   302→        if similar_count > 1:

   309→            )
   310→    if explain:
   311→        explanation = item.get("explain", {})
   312→        count_weight = explanation.get("count", int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0))
   313→        base = (
   314→            f"ranked by tier={tier}, confidence={confidence}, "
   315→            f"count={count_weight}, id={item.get('id', '')}"
   316→        )
   317→
   318→        # Add dimension context for mechanical detectors
   319→        if dim_scores and detector:
   320→            dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   321→            if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   322→                ds = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   323→                base += (
   324→                    f". Dimension: {dimension.name} at {ds['score']:.1f}% "
   325→                    f"({ds['issues']} open issues)"
   326→                )
   327→
   328→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   329→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   330→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   331→            if dim_key:
   332→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   333→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   334→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   335→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   336→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   337→                        break
   338→
   339→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   340→        if policy:
   341→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   342→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   343→
   344→
   345→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   346→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   347→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   348→        return
   349→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   350→    if not explain:
   351→        return
   352→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   353→    if available:
   354→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   355→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   356→
   357→
   358→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   359→    if queue.get("items"):
   360→        return False
   361→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   362→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   363→    if tier is not None:
   364→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   365→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   366→        if available:
   367→            commands = " | ".join(
   368→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   369→            )
   370→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   371→    return True
   372→
   373→
   374→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   375→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   376→    if not plan:
   377→        return
   378→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   379→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   380→    if not summary:
   381→        return
   382→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   383→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   384→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   385→
   386→
   387→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   388→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   389→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   390→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   391→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   392→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   393→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   394→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   395→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   396→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   397→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   398→
   399→    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
   400→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   401→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   402→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   403→    print()
   404→    if new:
   405→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   406→    if resolved:
   407→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   408→    if epic_count:
   409→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   410→    print()
   411→    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
   412→    print()
   413→    print("  Steps:")
   414→    print("    1. Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see all review findings")
   415→    print("    2. Analyze findings — group by root cause into clusters")
   416→    print("    3. Create clusters, set priorities, focus on first arc")
   417→    print('    4. Mark complete: `desloppify plan done synthesis::pending --note "..." --attest "..."`')
   418→    print("    5. THEN run `desloppify next` for implementation tasks")
   419→    print()
   420→    print("  Preview:  desloppify plan synthesize --dry-run")
   421→    print()
   422→    print("  View plan:")
   423→    print("    desloppify plan queue              — see all items in priority order")
   424→    print("    desloppify plan show               — plan status summary")
   425→
   426→
   427→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   428→    """Render an epic card."""
   429→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   430→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   431→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   432→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   433→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   434→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   435→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   436→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   437→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   438→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   439→
   440→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   441→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   442→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   443→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   444→    if root_cause:
   445→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   446→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   447→    if total_epics:
   448→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   449→    print()
   450→    if action_steps:
   451→        print("  Steps:")
   452→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   453→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   454→        print()
   455→    if finding_ids:
   456→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   457→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   458→        for fid in shown:
   459→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   460→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   461→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   462→        print()
   463→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   464→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   465→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   466→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   467→
   468→
   469→def render_terminal_items(
   470→    items: list[dict],
   471→    dim_scores: dict,
   472→    findings_scoped: dict,
   473→    *,
   474→    group: str,
   475→    explain: bool,
   476→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   477→    plan: dict | None = None,
   478→) -> None:
   479→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   480→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   481→
   482→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   483→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   484→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   485→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   486→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   487→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   488→        visible = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed")
   489→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({visible} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   490→
   491→        # Exclude the synthesis item from the focus count — it's a queue-level
   492→        # blocker, not a cluster member, so don't count it in "N of M remaining".
   493→        focus_items = [it for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed"]
   494→        if len(focus_items) == 0:
   495→            print(colorize(
   496→                "  This cluster has no remaining items. "
   497→                "Run `desloppify plan focus --clear` to unfocus, "
   498→                "or `desloppify plan cluster show` to inspect.",
   499→                "yellow",
   500→            ))
   501→
   502→    # When synthesis is pending, only show the planning card — don't leak
   503→    # implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip the planning step.
   504→    if items and items[0].get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   505→        queue_pos = items[0].get("queue_position")
   506→        pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   507→        print(colorize(f"  Next item{pos_str}", "bold"))
   508→        _render_synthesis_item(items[0])
   509→        return
   510→
   511→    if group != "item":
   512→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   513→        return
   514→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   515→        if idx > 0:
   516→            print()
   517→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   518→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   519→
   520→        # All item types get the same position label — no visual hierarchy
   521→        # that makes synthetic items look like metadata instead of tasks.
   522→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   523→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   524→        elif len(items) > 1:
   525→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   526→        else:
   527→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   528→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   529→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   530→
   531→        if kind == "epic":
   532→            _render_epic_item(item)
   533→            continue
   534→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   535→
   536→
   537→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   538→    if len(items) != 1:
   539→        return
   540→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   541→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   542→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   543→    if kind != "finding":
   544→        return
   545→    item = items[0]
   546→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   547→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   548→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   549→        primary = item.get(
   550→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   551→        )
   552→        print(f"    {primary}")
   553→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   554→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   555→        return
   556→
   557→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   558→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   559→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   560→        print(f"    {primary}")
   561→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   562→    else:
   563→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   564→
   565→    print(
   566→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   567→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   568→    )
   569→    print(
   570→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   571→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   572→    )
   573→
   574→
   575→def render_followup_nudges(
   576→    state: dict,
   577→    dim_scores: dict,
   578→    findings_scoped: dict,
   579→    *,
   580→    strict_score: float | None,
   581→    target_strict_score: float,
   582→) -> None:
   583→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   584→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   585→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   586→        state,
   587→        subjective_entries,
   588→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   589→        max_quality_items=3,
   590→        max_integrity_items=5,
   591→    )
   592→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   593→    if strict_score is not None:
   594→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   595→        if gap > 0:
   596→            print(
   597→                colorize(
   598→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   599→                    "cyan",
   600→                )
   601→            )
   602→        else:
   603→            print(
   604→                colorize(
   605→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   606→                    "green",
   607→                )
   608→            )
   609→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   610→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   611→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   612→
   613→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   614→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   615→        findings_scoped
   616→    )
   617→    parts: list[str] = []
   618→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   619→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   620→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   621→    open_review = [
   622→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   623→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   624→    ]
   625→    if low_dims:
   626→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   627→    if stale_count:
   628→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   629→    if unassessed_count:
   630→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   631→    if len(open_review):
   632→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   633→    if coverage_open > 0:
   634→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   635→
   636→    if parts:
   637→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   638→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   639→
   640→
   641→__all__ = [
   642→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   643→    "render_followup_nudges",
   644→    "render_queue_header",
   645→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   646→    "render_terminal_items",
   647→    "scorecard_subjective",
   648→    "show_empty_queue",
   649→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   650→]

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_work_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    59→            tier=tier_filter,
    60→            count=None,
    61→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    62→            status="open",
    63→            include_subjective=True,
    64→            plan=plan_data,
    65→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    66→            cluster=effective_cluster,
    67→            collapse_clusters=True,
    68→        ),
    69→    )
    70→    items = queue.get("items", [])
    71→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    72→
    73→    # Header
    74→    total = len(items)
    75→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    76→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    77→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    78→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    79→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    80→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    81→    else:
    82→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    83→
    84→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    85→    if focus:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    87→
    88→    if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    89→        # Check plan for skipped items not in queue
    90→        plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    91→        if plan_skipped_count:
    92→            print(colorize(
    93→                f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    94→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    95→                "dim",
    96→            ))
    97→
    98→    if not items:
    99→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    # Determine which items to show
   103→    display_items = items
   104→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   105→        display_items = items[:top]
   106→
   107→    # Build rows
   108→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   109→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   110→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   111→        pos = str(idx)
   112→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   113→
   114→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   115→            tier_str = "T1"
   116→            detector = "synthesis"
   117→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   118→            n = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   119→            summary = f"[synthesis] {n} findings need integration"
   120→            cluster_name = ""
   121→        elif kind == "epic":
   122→            tier_str = "T1"
   123→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   124→            direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   125→            fcount = len(detail.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→            status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   127→            detector = "epic"
   128→            summary = f"[{direction}] [{fcount}] [{status}] {detail.get('thesis', '')}"
   129→            cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   130→        elif kind == "cluster":
   131→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
   132→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   133→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   134→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   135→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   136→        else:
   137→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   138→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   139→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   140→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   141→            # Extract 8-char hash suffix so users can reference items by short ID
   142→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   143→            if "::" in item_id:
   144→                hash_suffix = item_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8]
   145→                # Truncate summary first, then append hash so it's never cut off
   146→                summary = f"{_truncate(summary, 36)} [{hash_suffix}]"
   147→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   148→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   149→
   150→        suffix = ""
   151→        if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   152→            suffix = " [skip]"
   153→
   154→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   155→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   156→
   157→    print()
   158→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 44, 24]
   159→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   160→
   161→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   162→        remaining = len(items) - top
   163→        print(colorize(
   164→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   165→        ))
   166→    print()
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   170→

    16→})
    17→
    18→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    19→
    20→
    21→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    22→    finding_id: Required[str]
    23→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    24→    reason: str | None
    25→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    26→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    27→    created_at: str
    28→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    29→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    30→    original_position: int | None  # queue position before skip (for restore)
    31→
    32→
    33→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    34→    finding_id: Required[str]
    35→    description: str | None
    36→    note: str | None
    37→    cluster: str | None
    38→    created_at: str
    39→    updated_at: str
    40→
    41→
    42→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    43→    name: Required[str]
    44→    description: str | None
    45→    finding_ids: list[str]
    46→    created_at: str
    47→    updated_at: str
    48→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    49→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    50→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    51→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    52→
    53→
    54→class Epic(TypedDict, total=False):
    55→    name: Required[str]
    56→    thesis: Required[str]
    57→    direction: Required[str]  # One of VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS
    58→    root_cause: str
    59→    finding_ids: list[str]
    60→    supersedes: list[str]
    61→    dismissed: list[str]
    62→    agent_safe: bool
    63→    dependency_order: int
    64→    action_steps: list[str]
    65→    source_clusters: list[str]
    66→    status: str  # "pending" | "in_progress" | "completed"
    67→    created_at: str
    68→    updated_at: str
    69→    synthesis_version: int
    70→    auto: bool  # Always True (LLM-generated)
    71→
    72→
    73→class EpicSynthesisMeta(TypedDict, total=False):
    74→    synthesized_ids: list[str]
    75→    last_run: str
    76→    version: int
    77→    dismissed_ids: list[str]
    78→    finding_snapshot_hash: str
    79→    strategy_summary: str
    80→    trigger: str
    81→
    82→
    83→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    84→    original_id: Required[str]
    85→    original_detector: str
    86→    original_file: str
    87→    original_summary: str
    88→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    89→    superseded_at: str
    90→    remapped_to: str | None
    91→    candidates: list[str]
    92→    note: str | None
    93→
    94→
    95→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    96→    version: Required[int]
    97→    created: Required[str]
    98→    updated: Required[str]
    99→    queue_order: list[str]
   100→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
   101→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
   102→    active_cluster: str | None
   103→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
   104→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
   105→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
   106→    epics: dict[str, Epic]
   107→    epic_synthesis_meta: EpicSynthesisMeta
   108→    deleted_clusters: list[str]  # cluster_keys of deleted auto-clusters
   109→
   110→
   111→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   112→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   113→    now = utc_now()
   114→    return {
   115→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   116→        "created": now,
   117→        "updated": now,
   118→        "queue_order": [],
   119→        "deferred": [],
   120→        "skipped": {},
   121→        "active_cluster": None,
   122→        "overrides": {},
   123→        "clusters": {},
   124→        "superseded": {},
   125→        "epics": {},
   126→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   127→        "deleted_clusters": [],
   128→    }
   129→
   130→
   131→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   132→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   133→
   134→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict).
   135→    """
   136→    defaults = empty_plan()
   137→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   138→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   139→
   140→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   141→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   142→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   143→        plan["deferred"] = []
   144→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   145→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   146→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   147→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   148→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   149→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   150→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   151→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   152→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epics"), dict):
   153→        plan["epics"] = {}
   154→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   155→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   156→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deleted_clusters"), list):
   157→        plan["deleted_clusters"] = []
   158→
   159→    # Migrate deferred → skipped (v1 → v2)
   160→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   161→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   162→    if deferred:
   163→        now = utc_now()
   164→        for fid in list(deferred):
   165→            if fid not in skipped:
   166→                skipped[fid] = {
   167→                    "finding_id": fid,
   168→                    "kind": "temporary",
   169→                    "reason": None,
   170→                    "note": None,
   171→                    "attestation": None,
   172→                    "created_at": now,
   173→                    "review_after": None,
   174→                    "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   175→                }
   176→        deferred.clear()
   177→
   178→    # Normalize cluster fields
   179→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   180→        if isinstance(cluster, dict):
   181→            if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   182→                cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   183→            cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   184→            cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   185→            cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   186→            cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   187→
   188→
   189→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   190→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   191→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   192→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   193→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   194→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   195→
   196→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   197→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   198→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   199→    if overlap:
   200→        raise ValueError(
   201→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   202→        )
   203→
   204→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   205→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   206→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   207→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   208→            raise ValueError(
   209→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   210→            )
   211→
   212→
   213→__all__ = [
   214→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   215→    "Epic",
   216→    "EpicSynthesisMeta",
   217→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   218→    "Cluster",
   219→    "ItemOverride",
   220→    "PlanModel",
   221→    "SkipEntry",
   222→    "SupersededEntry",
   223→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   224→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   225→    "empty_plan",
   226→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   227→    "validate_plan",
   228→]
   229→

1→"""Ranking and grouping helpers for work queue selection."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     6→    detail_dict,
     7→    is_review_finding,
     8→    is_subjective_finding,
     9→    primary_command_for_finding,
    10→    review_finding_weight,
    11→    scope_matches,
    12→    slugify,
    13→    status_matches,
    14→    subjective_strict_scores,
    15→    supported_fixers_for_item,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    88→
    89→    # Synthesis and epic items sort before everything else (T0-equivalent).
    90→    if kind == "synthesis_needed":
    91→        return (-2, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    92→    if kind == "epic":
    93→        dep_order = 999
    94→        detail = item.get("detail")
    95→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    96→            dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
    97→        return (-1, dep_order, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    98→
    99→    if kind == "cluster":
   100→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   101→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
   102→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
   103→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
   104→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   105→        )
   106→        return (
   107→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
   108→            action_pri,
   109→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   110→            item.get("id", ""),
   111→        )
   112→
   113→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   114→        return (
   115→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
   116→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
   117→            subjective_score_value(item),
   118→            item.get("id", ""),
   119→        )
   120→
   121→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   122→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   123→    return (
   124→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   125→        0,
   126→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   127→        -review_weight,
   128→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   129→        item.get("id", ""),
   130→    )
   131→
   132→
   133→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   134→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   135→
   136→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   137→        return {
   138→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   139→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   140→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   141→            "policy": (
   142→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   143→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   144→            ),
   145→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   146→        }
   147→
   148→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   149→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   150→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
   151→    is_review = bool(item.get("is_review"))
   152→    ranking_factors: list[str]
   153→    if is_subjective:
   154→        ranking_factors = ["tier fixed to T4", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"]
   155→    elif is_review:
   156→        ranking_factors = [
   157→            "tier asc",
   158→            "confidence asc",
   159→            "review_weight desc",
   160→            "count desc",
   161→            "id asc",
   162→        ]
   163→    else:
   164→        ranking_factors = ["tier asc", "confidence asc", "count desc", "id asc"]
   165→    explain = {
   166→        "kind": "finding",
   167→        "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   168→        "confidence": confidence,
   169→        "confidence_rank": CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(confidence, 9),
   170→        "count": int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   171→        "id": item.get("id", ""),
   172→        "ranking_factors": ranking_factors,
   173→    }
   174→    if is_review:
   175→        explain["review_weight"] = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   176→    if is_subjective:
   177→        explain["policy"] = (
   178→            "Subjective findings are forced to T4 and do not outrank "
   179→            "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   180→        )
   181→        explain["subjective_score"] = subjective_score_value(item)
   182→    return explain
   183→
   184→
   185→def tier_counts(items: list[dict]) -> dict[int, int]:
   186→    counts = {1: 0, 2: 0, 3: 0, 4: 0}
   187→    for item in items:
   188→        if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   189→            # Count each member at its own tier
   190→            for member in item.get("members", []):
   191→                tier = int(member.get("effective_tier", member.get("tier", 3)))
   192→                counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   193→            continue
   194→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   195→        counts[tier] = counts.get(tier, 0) + 1
   196→    return counts
   197→
   198→
   199→def choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier: int, counts: dict[int, int]) -> int | None:
   200→    available = [tier for tier, count in counts.items() if count > 0]
   201→    if not available:
   202→        return None
   203→    return min(available, key=lambda tier: (abs(tier - requested_tier), tier))
   204→
   205→
   206→def group_queue_items(items: list[dict], group: str) -> dict[str, list[dict]]:
   207→    """Group queue items for alternate output modes."""
   208→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   209→    for item in items:
   210→        if group == "file":
   211→            key = item.get("file", "")
   212→        elif group == "detector":
   213→            key = item.get("detector", "")
   214→        elif group == "tier":
   215→            key = f"T{int(item.get('effective_tier', item.get('tier', 3)))}"
   216→        elif group == "cluster":
   217→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   218→            key = plan_cluster["name"] if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else "(unclustered)"
   219→        else:
   220→            key = "items"
   221→        grouped.setdefault(key, []).append(item)
   222→    return grouped
   223→
   224→
   225→__all__ = [
   226→    "build_finding_items",
   227→    "choose_fallback_tier",
   228→    "item_explain",
   229→    "item_sort_key",
   230→    "tier_counts",
   231→    "subjective_score_value",
   232→    "group_queue_items",
   233→]
   234→

1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

     5→import argparse

     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.next_parts import output as next_output_mod

    28→
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    76→    if config_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    80→
    81→
    82→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    83→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    84→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    85→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    86→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    87→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    88→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    89→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    90→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    91→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    92→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    93→
    94→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    95→
    96→    # Load the living plan
    97→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    98→    plan = load_plan()
    99→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   100→    if (
   101→        plan.get("queue_order")
   102→        or plan.get("overrides")
   103→        or plan.get("clusters")
   104→    ):
   105→        plan_data = plan
   106→
   107→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   109→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   110→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   111→        if active_cluster:
   112→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   113→
   114→    queue = build_work_queue(
   115→        state,
   116→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   117→            tier=tier,
   118→            count=count,
   119→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   120→            scope=scope,
   121→            status=status,
   122→            include_subjective=True,
   123→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   124→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   125→            explain=explain,
   126→            plan=plan_data,
   127→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   128→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   129→        ),
   130→    )
   131→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   141→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   142→    )
   143→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   144→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   145→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   147→        state,
   148→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   149→    )
   150→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   151→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   152→    ]
   153→    write_query(payload)
   154→
   155→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   156→    if output_file:
   157→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   158→            output_file,
   159→            payload,
   160→            len(items),
   161→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   162→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   163→        ):
   164→            return
   165→        raise SystemExit(1)
   166→
   167→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   168→        return
   169→
   170→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   171→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   172→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(queue, tier, strict_score):
   173→        return
   174→
   175→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   176→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   177→        state.get("findings", {}),
   178→        state.get("scan_path"),
   179→    )
   180→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   181→    try:
   182→        from desloppify.scoring import merge_potentials
   183→        potentials = merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   184→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        potentials = raw_potentials or None
   186→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   187→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   188→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   189→    )
   190→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   191→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   192→        state,
   193→        dim_scores,
   194→        findings_scoped,
   195→        strict_score=strict_score,
   196→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   197→    )
   198→    print()
   199→
   200→
   201→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
   202→

1→"""plan command: generate prioritized markdown plan from state.
     2→
     3→This module preserves the original import path. The full plan command
     4→(with subcommands) lives in ``desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd``.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import argparse
    10→
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_agent_plan
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    14→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import warn_best_effort
    15→from desloppify.engine import planning as planning_mod
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.tooling import check_config_staleness
    19→
    20→
    21→def cmd_plan_output(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    22→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state."""
    23→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    24→    state = runtime.state
    25→
    26→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    27→        return
    28→
    29→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(runtime.config)
    30→    if config_warning:
    31→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    32→
    33→    plan_md = planning_mod.generate_plan_md(state)
    34→    next_command = "desloppify next --count 20"
    35→
    36→    output = getattr(args, "output", None)
    37→    if output:
    38→        try:
    39→            safe_write_text(output, plan_md)
    40→            print(colorize(f"Plan written to {output}", "green"))
    41→            print_agent_plan(
    42→                ["Inspect and execute the generated plan."],
    43→                next_command=next_command,
    44→            )
    45→        except OSError as e:
    46→            warn_best_effort(f"Could not write plan to {output}: {e}")
    47→    else:
    48→        print(plan_md)
    49→        print()
    50→        print_agent_plan(
    51→            ["Start from the top-ranked action in this plan."],
    52→            next_command=next_command,
    53→        )
    54→
    55→
    56→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_output"]
    57→
