1→"""Markdown plan rendering."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections import defaultdict
     6→from datetime import date
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import dimension_action_type
     9→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import TIER_LABELS
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.types import PlanState
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→from desloppify.scoring import DIMENSIONS, DISPLAY_NAMES
    13→from desloppify.state import get_objective_score, get_overall_score, get_strict_score
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    15→
    16→
    17→def _plan_header(state: PlanState, stats: dict) -> list[str]:
    18→    """Build the plan header: title, score line, and codebase metrics."""
    19→    overall_score = get_overall_score(state)
    20→    objective_score = get_objective_score(state)
    21→    strict_score = get_strict_score(state)
    22→
    23→    if (
    24→        overall_score is not None
    25→        and objective_score is not None
    26→        and strict_score is not None
    27→    ):
    28→        header_score = (
    29→            f"**Health:** overall {overall_score:.1f}/100 | "
    30→            f"objective {objective_score:.1f}/100 | "
    31→            f"strict {strict_score:.1f}/100"
    32→        )
    33→    elif overall_score is not None:
    34→        header_score = f"**Score: {overall_score:.1f}/100**"
    35→    else:
    36→        header_score = "**Scores unavailable**"
    37→
    38→    metrics = state.get("codebase_metrics", {})
    39→    total_files = sum(metric.get("total_files", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    40→    total_loc = sum(metric.get("total_loc", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    41→    total_dirs = sum(metric.get("total_directories", 0) for metric in metrics.values())
    42→
    43→    lines = [
    44→        f"# Desloppify Plan — {date.today().isoformat()}",
    45→        "",
    46→        f"{header_score} | "
    47→        f"{stats.get('open', 0)} open | "
    48→        f"{stats.get('fixed', 0)} fixed | "
    49→        f"{stats.get('wontfix', 0)} wontfix | "
    50→        f"{stats.get('auto_resolved', 0)} auto-resolved",
    51→        "",
    52→    ]
    53→
    54→    if total_files:
    55→        loc_str = (
    56→            f"{total_loc:,}"
    57→            if total_loc < LOC_COMPACT_THRESHOLD
    58→            else f"{total_loc // 1000}K"
    59→        )
    60→        lines.append(
    61→            f"\n{total_files} files · {loc_str} LOC · {total_dirs} directories\n"
    62→        )
    63→
    64→    return lines
    65→
    66→
    67→def _plan_dimension_table(state: PlanState) -> list[str]:
    68→    """Build the dimension health table rows (empty list when no data)."""
    69→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
    70→    if not dim_scores:
    71→        return []
    72→
    73→    lines = [
    74→        "## Health by Dimension",
    75→        "",
    76→        "| Dimension | Tier | Checks | Issues | Health | Strict | Action |",
    77→        "|-----------|------|--------|--------|--------|--------|--------|",
    78→    ]
    79→    static_names: set[str] = set()
    80→    rendered_names: set[str] = set()
    81→    subjective_display_names = {
    82→        display.lower() for display in DISPLAY_NAMES.values()
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    def _looks_subjective(name: str, data: dict) -> bool:
    86→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
    87→        if "subjective_assessment" in detectors:
    88→            return True
    89→        lowered = name.strip().lower()
    90→        return lowered in subjective_display_names or lowered.startswith("elegance")
    91→
    92→    for dim in DIMENSIONS:
    93→        ds = dim_scores.get(dim.name)
    94→        if not ds:
    95→            continue
    96→        static_names.add(dim.name)
    97→        rendered_names.add(dim.name)
    98→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
    99→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   100→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   101→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   102→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   103→        action = dimension_action_type(dim.name)
   104→        lines.append(
   105→            f"| {bold}{dim.name}{bold} | T{dim.tier} | "
   106→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   107→        )
   108→
   109→    from desloppify.engine.planning.dimension_rows import scorecard_dimension_rows
   110→
   111→    scorecard_rows = scorecard_dimension_rows(state)
   112→    scorecard_subjective_rows = [
   113→        (name, ds) for name, ds in scorecard_rows if _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   114→    ]
   115→    scorecard_subjective_names = {name for name, _ in scorecard_subjective_rows}
   116→
   117→    # Show custom dimensions not present in scorecard.png in the main table.
   118→    custom_non_subjective_rows: list[tuple[str, dict]] = []
   119→    for name, ds in sorted(dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()):
   120→        if name in rendered_names or not isinstance(ds, dict):
   121→            continue
   122→        if _looks_subjective(name, ds):
   123→            continue
   124→        custom_non_subjective_rows.append((name, ds))
   125→        rendered_names.add(name)
   126→
   127→    for name, ds in custom_non_subjective_rows:
   128→        checks = ds.get("checks", 0)
   129→        issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   130→        score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   131→        strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   132→        tier = int(ds.get("tier", 3) or 3)
   133→        bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   134→        action = dimension_action_type(name)
   135→        lines.append(
   136→            f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   137→            f"{checks:,} | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | {action} |"
   138→        )
   139→
   140→    extra_subjective_rows = [
   141→        (name, ds)
   142→        for name, ds in sorted(
   143→            dim_scores.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]).lower()
   144→        )
   145→        if (
   146→            isinstance(ds, dict)
   147→            and name not in scorecard_subjective_names
   148→            and name.strip().lower() not in subjective_display_names
   149→            and name.strip().lower() not in {"elegance", "elegance (combined)"}
   150→            and _looks_subjective(name, ds)
   151→        )
   152→    ]
   153→    subjective_rows = [*scorecard_subjective_rows, *extra_subjective_rows]
   154→
   155→    if subjective_rows:
   156→        lines.append("| **Subjective Measures (matches scorecard.png)** | | | | | | |")
   157→        for name, ds in subjective_rows:
   158→            issues = ds.get("issues", 0)
   159→            score_val = ds.get("score", 100)
   160→            strict_val = ds.get("strict", score_val)
   161→            tier = ds.get("tier", 4)
   162→            bold = "**" if score_val < 93 else ""
   163→            lines.append(
   164→                f"| {bold}{name}{bold} | T{tier} | "
   165→                f"— | {issues} | {score_val:.1f}% | {strict_val:.1f}% | review |"
   166→            )
   167→
   168→    lines.append("")
   169→    return lines
   170→
   171→
   172→def _plan_tier_sections(findings: dict, *, state: PlanState | None = None) -> list[str]:
   173→    """Build per-tier sections from the shared work-queue backend."""
   174→
   175→    queue_state: PlanState | dict = state or {"findings": findings}
   176→    scan_path = state.get("scan_path") if state else None
   177→    raw_target = (
   178→        (state or {}).get("config", {}).get("target_strict_score", 95)
   179→        if isinstance(state, dict)
   180→        else 95
   181→    )
   182→    try:
   183→        subjective_threshold = float(raw_target)
   184→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   185→        subjective_threshold = 95.0
   186→    subjective_threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold))
   187→    if "findings" not in queue_state:
   188→        queue_state = {**queue_state, "findings": findings}
   189→
   190→    queue = build_work_queue(
   191→        queue_state,
   192→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   193→            count=None,
   194→            scan_path=scan_path,
   195→            status="open",
   196→            include_subjective=True,
   197→            subjective_threshold=subjective_threshold,
   198→            no_tier_fallback=True,
   199→        ),
   200→    )
   201→    open_items = queue.get("items", [])
   202→    by_tier_file: dict[int, dict[str, list]] = defaultdict(lambda: defaultdict(list))
   203→    for item in open_items:
   204→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   205→        by_tier_file[tier][item.get("file", ".")].append(item)
   206→
   207→    lines: list[str] = []
   208→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   209→        tier_files = by_tier_file.get(tier_num, {})
   210→        if not tier_files:
   211→            continue
   212→
   213→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   214→        tier_count = sum(len(file_findings) for file_findings in tier_files.values())
   215→        lines.extend(
   216→            [
   217→                "---",
   218→                f"## Tier {tier_num}: {label} ({tier_count} open)",
   219→                "",
   220→            ]
   221→        )
   222→
   223→        sorted_files = sorted(
   224→            tier_files.items(), key=lambda item: (-len(item[1]), item[0])
   225→        )
   226→        for filepath, file_items in sorted_files:
   227→            display_path = "Codebase-wide" if filepath == "." else filepath
   228→            lines.append(f"### `{display_path}` ({len(file_items)} findings)")
   229→            lines.append("")
   230→            for item in file_items:
   231→                if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   232→                    lines.append(f"- [ ] [subjective] {item.get('summary', '')}")
   233→                    lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   234→                    if item.get("primary_command"):
   235→                        lines.append(f"      action: `{item['primary_command']}`")
   236→                    continue
   237→
   238→                conf_badge = f"[{item.get('confidence', 'medium')}]"
   239→                lines.append(f"- [ ] {conf_badge} {item.get('summary', '')}")
   240→                lines.append(f"      `{item.get('id', '')}`")
   241→            lines.append("")
   242→
   243→    return lines
   244→
   245→
   246→def _tier_summary_lines(stats: dict) -> list[str]:
   247→    lines: list[str] = []
   248→    by_tier = stats.get("by_tier", {})
   249→    for tier_num in [1, 2, 3, 4]:
   250→        tier_stats = by_tier.get(str(tier_num), {})
   251→        open_count = tier_stats.get("open", 0)
   252→        total = sum(tier_stats.values())
   253→        addressed = total - open_count
   254→        pct = round(addressed / total * 100) if total else 100
   255→        label = TIER_LABELS.get(tier_num, f"Tier {tier_num}")
   256→        lines.append(
   257→            f"- **Tier {tier_num}** ({label}): {open_count} open / {total} total ({pct}% addressed)"
   258→        )
   259→    lines.append("")
   260→    return lines
   261→
   262→
   263→def _addressed_section(findings: dict) -> list[str]:
   264→    addressed = [
   265→        finding for finding in findings.values() if finding["status"] != "open"
   266→    ]
   267→    if not addressed:
   268→        return []
   269→
   270→    lines: list[str] = ["---", "## Addressed", ""]
   271→    by_status: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
   272→    for finding in addressed:
   273→        by_status[finding["status"]] += 1
   274→    for status, count in sorted(by_status.items()):
   275→        lines.append(f"- **{status}**: {count}")
   276→
   277→    wontfix = [
   278→        finding
   279→        for finding in addressed
   280→        if finding["status"] == "wontfix" and finding.get("note")
   281→    ]
   282→    if wontfix:
   283→        lines.extend(["", "### Wontfix (with explanations)", ""])
   284→        for finding in wontfix[:30]:
   285→            lines.append(f"- `{finding['id']}` — {finding['note']}")
   286→
   287→    lines.append("")
   288→    return lines
   289→
   290→
   291→def _plan_user_ordered_section(
   292→    items: list[dict],
   293→    plan: dict,
   294→) -> list[str]:
   295→    """Render the user-ordered queue section, grouped by cluster."""
   296→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   297→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   298→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   299→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   300→
   301→    ordered_ids = set(queue_order) - skipped_ids
   302→    if not ordered_ids:
   303→        return []
   304→
   305→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   306→
   307→    lines: list[str] = [
   308→        "---",
   309→        f"## User-Ordered Queue ({len(ordered_ids)} items)",
   310→        "",
   311→    ]
   312→
   313→    # Group by cluster: clustered items first, then unclustered
   314→    emitted: set[str] = set()
   315→    for cluster_name, cluster in clusters.items():
   316→        member_ids = [
   317→            fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   318→            if fid in ordered_ids and fid in by_id
   319→        ]
   320→        if not member_ids:
   321→            continue
   322→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   323→        lines.append(f"### Cluster: {cluster_name}")
   324→        if desc:
   325→            lines.append(f"> {desc}")
   326→        lines.append("")
   327→        for fid in member_ids:
   328→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   329→            if item:
   330→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   331→                emitted.add(fid)
   332→        lines.append("")
   333→
   334→    # Unclustered ordered items
   335→    unclustered = [
   336→        fid for fid in queue_order
   337→        if fid in ordered_ids and fid not in emitted and fid in by_id
   338→    ]
   339→    if unclustered:
   340→        if any(c.get("finding_ids") for c in clusters.values()):
   341→            lines.append("### (unclustered ordered items)")
   342→            lines.append("")
   343→        for fid in unclustered:
   344→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   345→            if item:
   346→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   347→        lines.append("")
   348→
   349→    return lines
   350→
   351→
   352→def _plan_skipped_section(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   353→    """Render the skipped items section, grouped by kind."""
   354→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   355→    if not skipped:
   356→        return []
   357→
   358→    by_id = {item.get("id"): item for item in items}
   359→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   360→
   361→    # Group by kind
   362→    by_kind: dict[str, list[str]] = {"temporary": [], "permanent": [], "false_positive": []}
   363→    for fid, entry in skipped.items():
   364→        kind = entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   365→        by_kind.setdefault(kind, []).append(fid)
   366→
   367→    kind_labels = {
   368→        "temporary": "Skipped Temporarily",
   369→        "permanent": "Wontfix (permanent)",
   370→        "false_positive": "False Positives",
   371→    }
   372→
   373→    lines: list[str] = [
   374→        "---",
   375→        f"## Skipped ({len(skipped)} items)",
   376→        "",
   377→    ]
   378→
   379→    for kind in ("temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"):
   380→        ids = by_kind.get(kind, [])
   381→        if not ids:
   382→            continue
   383→        lines.append(f"### {kind_labels[kind]} ({len(ids)})")
   384→        lines.append("")
   385→        for fid in ids:
   386→            entry = skipped.get(fid, {})
   387→            item = by_id.get(fid)
   388→            if item:
   389→                lines.extend(_render_plan_item(item, overrides.get(fid, {})))
   390→            else:
   391→                lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~ (not in current queue)")
   392→            reason = entry.get("reason")
   393→            if reason:
   394→                lines.append(f"      Reason: {reason}")
   395→            note = entry.get("note")
   396→            if note and not overrides.get(fid, {}).get("note"):
   397→                lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   398→            review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   399→            if review_after:
   400→                skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
```

   406→
   407→def _plan_superseded_section(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   408→    """Render the superseded items section."""
   409→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   410→    if not superseded:
   411→        return []
   412→
   413→    lines: list[str] = [
   414→        "---",
   415→        f"## Superseded ({len(superseded)} items — may need remap)",
   416→        "",
   417→    ]
   418→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   419→        summary = entry.get("original_summary", "")
   420→        summary_str = f" — {summary}" if summary else ""
   421→        lines.append(f"- ~~{fid}~~{summary_str}")
   422→        candidates = entry.get("candidates", [])
   423→        if candidates:
   424→            lines.append(f"  Candidates: {', '.join(candidates[:3])}")
   425→        note = entry.get("note")
   426→        if note:
   427→            lines.append(f"  Note: {note}")
   428→    lines.append("")
   429→    return lines
   430→
   431→
   432→def _render_plan_item(item: dict, override: dict) -> list[str]:
   433→    """Render a single plan item as markdown lines."""
   434→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   435→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   436→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
   437→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
   438→
   439→    lines = [f"- [ ] [T{tier}/{confidence}] {summary}"]
   440→    desc = override.get("description")
   441→    if desc:
   442→        lines.append(f"      → {desc}")
   443→    lines.append(f"      `{item_id}`")
   444→    note = override.get("note")
   445→    if note:
   446→        lines.append(f"      Note: {note}")
   447→    return lines
   448→
   449→
   450→def generate_plan_md(state: PlanState, plan: dict | None = None) -> str:
   451→    """Generate a prioritized markdown plan from state.
   452→
   453→    When *plan* is provided (or auto-loaded from disk), user-ordered
   454→    items, clusters, skipped, and superseded sections are rendered.
   455→    When no plan exists, output is identical to the previous behavior.
   456→    """
   457→    findings = state["findings"]
   458→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   459→
   460→    # Auto-load plan if not provided
   461→    if plan is None:
   462→        try:
   463→            from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   464→            plan = load_plan()
   465→        except Exception:
   466→            plan = {}
   467→
   468→    has_plan = bool(
   469→        plan
   470→        and (
   471→            plan.get("queue_order")
   472→            or plan.get("skipped")
   473→            or plan.get("clusters")
   474→        )
   475→    )
   476→
   477→    lines = _plan_header(state, stats)
   478→    lines.extend(_plan_dimension_table(state))
   479→    lines.extend(_tier_summary_lines(stats))
   480→
   481→    if has_plan:
   482→        # Build full queue for item lookup
   483→        queue = build_work_queue(
   484→            state,
   485→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
   486→                count=None,
   487→                scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   488→                status="open",
   489→                include_subjective=True,
   490→                no_tier_fallback=True,
   491→            ),
   492→        )
   493→        all_items = queue.get("items", [])
   494→        lines.extend(_plan_user_ordered_section(all_items, plan))
   495→
   496→        # Remaining: items NOT in queue_order or skipped
   497→        ordered_ids = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   498→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   499→        plan_ids = ordered_ids | skipped_ids
   500→        remaining = [item for item in all_items if item.get("id") not in plan_ids]
   501→        if remaining:
   502→            lines.append("---")
   503→            lines.append(f"## Remaining (mechanical order, {len(remaining)} items)")
   504→            lines.append("")
   505→
   506→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   507→        lines.extend(_plan_skipped_section(all_items, plan))
   508→        lines.extend(_plan_superseded_section(plan))
   509→    else:
   510→        lines.extend(_plan_tier_sections(findings, state=state))
   511→
   512→    lines.extend(_addressed_section(findings))
   513→
   514→    return "\n".join(lines)
   515→

1→"""Reusable CLI rendering snippets for command modules."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def print_agent_plan(
    12→    steps: list[str],
    13→    *,
    14→    next_command: str | None = None,
    15→    header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:",
    16→    plan: dict | None = None,
    17→) -> None:
    18→    """Print a consistent AGENT PLAN block with numbered steps.
    19→
    20→    When a living *plan* is active, renders plan focus/progress instead
    21→    of the narrative-derived steps.
    22→    """
    23→    if plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters")):
    24→        _print_plan_agent_block(plan, header=header)
    25→        return
    26→    if not steps:
    27→        return
    28→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    29→    for idx, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
    30→        print(colorize(f"  {idx}. {step}", "dim"))
    31→    if next_command:
    32→        print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
    33→
    34→
    35→def _print_plan_agent_block(plan: dict, *, header: str = "  AGENT PLAN:") -> None:
    36→    """Render the living plan as the agent plan block."""
    37→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    38→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    39→    skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    40→
    41→    print(colorize(header, "yellow"))
    42→    print(colorize(f"  Living plan active: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.", "dim"))
    43→    if active:
    44→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    45→        remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    46→        print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).", "dim"))
    47→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
    48→    if epics:
    49→        active_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") != "completed")
    50→        print(colorize(f"  Epics: {len(epics)} ({active_epics} active).", "dim"))
    51→        meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    52→        strategy = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
    53→        if strategy:
    54→            short = strategy.split(".")[0] + "." if "." in strategy else strategy
    55→            print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {short}", "dim"))
    56→    print(colorize("  Next command: `desloppify next`", "dim"))
    57→    print(colorize("  View plan: `desloppify plan`", "dim"))
    58→
    59→
    60→def print_replacement_groups(
    61→    groups: dict[str, list[tuple[str, str]]],
    62→    *,
    63→    title: str,
    64→    rel_fn: Callable[[str], str] = rel,
    65→) -> None:
    66→    """Print grouped old→new replacement lines by file."""
    67→    if not groups:
    68→        return
    69→    print(colorize(title, "cyan"))
    70→    for filepath, replacements in sorted(groups.items()):
    71→        print(f"    {rel_fn(filepath)}:")
    72→        for old, new in replacements:
    73→            print(f"      {old}  →  {new}")
    74→    print()
    75→
    76→
    77→def print_ranked_actions(
    78→    actions: list[dict], *, limit: int = 3, plan: dict | None = None
    79→) -> bool:
    80→    """Print the highest-impact narrative actions and return True when shown."""
    81→    ranked = sorted(
    82→        [action for action in actions if int(action.get("count", 0)) > 0],
    83→        key=lambda action: (
    84→            -float(action.get("impact", 0.0)),
    85→            -int(action.get("count", 0)),
    86→            int(action.get("priority", 999)),
    87→        ),
    88→    )
    89→    if not ranked:
    90→        return False
    91→    has_plan = plan and (plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("clusters"))
    92→    label = "Score context:" if has_plan else "Biggest things impacting score:"
    93→    print(colorize(f"  {label}", "cyan"))
    94→    for action in ranked[:limit]:
    95→        detector = action.get("detector", "unknown")
    96→        count = int(action.get("count", 0))
    97→        cluster_count = action.get("cluster_count")
    98→        if cluster_count:
    99→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open in {cluster_count} cluster(s) — `desloppify next`", "dim"))
   100→        else:
   101→            command = action.get("command", "desloppify next")
   102→            print(colorize(f"    - {detector}: {count} open — `{command}`", "dim"))
   103→    return True
   104→
   105→
   106→__all__ = [
   107→    "print_agent_plan",
   108→    "print_ranked_actions",
   109→    "print_replacement_groups",
   110→]
   111→

1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→
```

## Summary: How `desloppify plan` and `desloppify next` Render Output

Based on my thorough analysis of the rendering logic across these key files, here's how the top-ranked items are presented to the user:

### 1. **First/Top Item Presentation: CLEARLY ACTIONABLE TASK**

The first item is presented as a **concrete, actionable task** — NOT a category header or metadata label. Here's how:

#### In `desloppify next` (single item, default):
From `/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py` lines 520-534:

```python
if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
    label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
elif len(items) > 1:
    label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
else:
    pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
    label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
print(colorize(label, "bold"))
```

**When showing one item, it literally says**: `Next item` (or `Next item (#<N> in queue)`)

Then immediately shows:
- Tier and confidence badge: `(Tier 1, high confidence)` (line 164)
- Summary as yellow text: `{item.get('summary', '')}` (line 166)
- File path, ID, code snippet, dimension scores, impact estimate, etc.

#### In `desloppify plan queue` (table view):
From `/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py` lines 108-155:

Items are rendered in a **numbered table** where:
- Column 1: `#` (position, e.g., "1" for top item)
- Column 2: Tier (e.g., "T1")
- Column 3: Detector (e.g., "unused_import")
- Column 4: Summary (e.g., "Unused import 'foo'")
- Column 5: Cluster (if part of a cluster)

**Position #1 in the table is clearly a specific finding, not a header.**

### 2. **How Individual Items Are Distinguished from Grouping/Epic Headers**

Items have different visual treatment based on their `kind` field (from `/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` and ranking logic):

#### **Individual Finding** (`kind: "finding"`):
- Simple checkbox line in markdown: `- [ ] [T1/high] Summary text`
- ID shown on next line: `` `finding-id` ``
- In terminal: tier, confidence, summary, file, dimensions (lines 153-342 in render.py)

#### **Cluster Meta-Item** (`kind: "cluster"`):
- Clearly marked header: `(Auto-fixable batch, 47 findings)` (line 111 in render.py)
- **Horizontal separator**: `─ ─ ─ ─` (line 112)
- Member breakdown by file
- Sample IDs shown (not the actual task)
- Cluster-specific action command (line 143)
- Resolution hints for "resolve all" (lines 146-150)

#### **Epic** (`kind: "epic"`):
- Clearly marked: `[Epic — delete] (42 findings, agent-safe, dependency_order: 1)` (line 441)
- **Horizontal separator**
- Thesis, root cause, status, action steps shown
- NOT presented as a single actionable task
- User must run `desloppify plan focus epic_name` first (lines 464-466)

#### **Synthesis Needed** (`kind: "synthesis_needed"`):
- Clearly marked: `PLAN — do not proceed until complete` (line 399)
- **Horizontal separator**
- Multi-paragraph explanation (lines 401-424)
- Explicit warning: "This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code" (line 411)
- Shows as a BLOCKER, not a regular task

### 3. **Actual Rendered Output Examples**

#### Terminal output for a single finding (lines 153-216):
```
  Next item (#1 in queue)
  (Tier 1, high confidence)
  ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Unused import 'foo' never used
  Type: Auto-fixable
  File: mymodule.py
  ID:   finding::unused_import::abc12345
  Lines: 42
  Category: unused_imports

  Code:
    import foo  # <-- line 42

  Dimension: Code Cleanliness — 67.3% (strict: 65.2%) (12 of 142 checks failing)
  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+2.1 pts on overall score
```

#### Terminal output for a cluster item (lines 100-150):
```
  (Auto-fixable batch, 47 findings)
  ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  Fix unused imports across the codebase

  Files:
    mymodule.py (8)
    other.py (5)
    ... and 3 more files

  Sample:
    - finding::unused_import::abc12345
    - finding::unused_import::def67890
    ... and 45 more

  Action: desloppify fix unused_imports --dry-run
```

#### Markdown plan output (lines 439-447):
```markdown
- [ ] [T1/high] Unused import 'foo' never used
      `finding::unused_import::abc12345`
      
- [ ] [T2/medium] Dead code function unused_handler
      `finding::dead_code::xyz78901`
      Note: Refactor to consolidate handlers
```

### 4. **Key Distinguishing Features**

| Element | Individual Finding | Cluster | Epic | Synthesis |
|---------|-------------------|---------|------|-----------|
| Opening label | "Tier X, confidence" | "(Batch type, # findings)" | "[Epic — direction]" | "PLAN — do not proceed" |
| Visual separator | Single dash line | Dash line | Dash line | Dash line |
| Content shows | Single specific issue | Member breakdown | Thesis + direction | Planning instructions |
| Primary command | Specific to finding OR bulk fix | Bulk command for cluster | None (requires focus first) | Manual planning steps |
| Presentation | "This specific code issue" | "These grouped related issues" | "This multi-issue epic" | "Blocking planning task" |

### 5. **Ranking/Sort Order (from `/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py` lines 86-130)**

The first item is determined by this priority:
1. **synthesis_needed** items (sort key: `-2`) — blocker
2. **Epic** items (sort key: `-1`, then by `dependency_order`)
3. **Cluster** items (sort key: `0`, then by action type priority)
4. **Individual findings** (sort key: tier, then confidence, then count)

**So the absolute first item is either:**
- A synthesis-needed blocker (planning task)
- An epic (multi-finding grouped challenge)
- A cluster (multiple auto-fixable items grouped)
- A single finding (one specific code issue)

**All are presented as actionable items with their own specific rendering**, never as ambiguous headers.

The key insight: **desloppify presents everything in the queue as an actionable unit at the appropriate level of granularity**. A cluster isn't presented as "category header" — it's presented as "here's a batch of related findings you can fix together with this command." An epic isn't a section marker — it's "here's a multi-finding challenge with a strategic direction and steps."

1→<!-- desloppify-begin -->
     2→<!-- desloppify-skill-version: 3 -->
     3→---
     4→name: desloppify
     5→description: >
     6→  Codebase health scanner and technical debt tracker. Use when the user asks
     7→  about code quality, technical debt, dead code, large files, god classes,
     8→  duplicate functions, code smells, naming issues, import cycles, or coupling
     9→  problems. Also use when asked for a health score, what to fix next, or to
    10→  create a cleanup plan. Supports 28 languages.
    11→allowed-tools: Bash(desloppify *)
    12→---
    13→
    14→# Desloppify
    15→
    16→## 1. Your Job
    17→
    18→Improve code quality by maximising the **strict score** honestly.
    19→
    20→**The main thing you do is run `desloppify next`** — it tells you exactly what to fix and how. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Keep going.
    21→
    22→**Read the output of every command** — it prints guidance, warnings, and next steps. Follow what it says rather than substituting your own analysis.
    23→
    24→## 2. The Workflow
    25→
    26→Two loops. The **outer loop** rescans periodically to measure progress.
    27→The **inner loop** is where you spend most of your time: fixing issues one by one.
    28→
    29→### Outer loop — scan and check
    30→
    31→```bash
    32→desloppify scan --path .       # analyse the codebase
    33→desloppify status              # check scores — are we at target?
    34→```
    35→If not at target, work the inner loop. Rescan periodically — especially after clearing a cluster or batch of related fixes. Issues cascade-resolve and new ones may surface.
    36→
    37→### Inner loop — fix issues
    38→
    39→Repeat until the queue is clear:
    40→
    41→```
    42→1. desloppify next              ← tells you exactly what to fix next
    43→2. Fix the issue in code
    44→3. Resolve it (next shows you the exact command including required attestation)
    45→```
    46→
    47→Score may temporarily drop after fixes — cascade effects are normal, keep going.
    48→If `next` suggests an auto-fixer, run `desloppify fix <fixer> --dry-run` to preview, then apply.
    49→
    50→**To be strategic**, use `plan` to shape what `next` gives you:
    51→```bash
    52→desloppify plan                        # see the full ordered queue
    53→desloppify plan move <pat> top         # reorder — what unblocks the most?
    54→desloppify plan cluster create <name>  # group related issues to batch-fix
    55→desloppify plan focus <cluster>        # scope next to one cluster
    56→desloppify plan defer <pat>            # push low-value items aside
    57→desloppify plan skip <pat>             # hide from next
    58→desloppify plan done <pat>             # mark complete
    59→desloppify plan synthesize             # group review findings into strategic epics
    60→desloppify plan reopen <pat>           # reopen
    61→```
    62→
    63→### Subjective reviews
    64→
    65→The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions.
    66→If you need to trigger one manually:
    67→```bash
    68→desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
    69→```
    70→
    71→### Other useful commands
    72→
    73→```bash
    74→desloppify next --count 5                         # top 5 priorities
    75→desloppify next --cluster <name>                  # drill into a cluster
    76→desloppify show <pattern>                         # filter by file/detector/ID
    77→desloppify show --status open                     # all open findings
    78→desloppify plan skip --permanent "<id>" --note "reason" # accept debt (lowers strict score)
    79→desloppify scan --path . --reset-subjective       # reset subjective baseline to 0
    80→```
    81→
    82→## 3. Reference
    83→
    84→### How scoring works
    85→
    86→Overall score = **40% mechanical** + **60% subjective**.
    87→
    88→- **Mechanical (40%)**: auto-detected issues — duplication, dead code, smells, unused imports, security. Fixed by changing code and rescanning.
    89→- **Subjective (60%)**: design quality review — naming, error handling, abstractions, clarity. Starts at **0%** until reviewed. The scan will prompt you when a review is needed.
    90→- **Strict score** is the north star: wontfix items count as open. The gap between overall and strict is your wontfix debt.
    91→- **Score types**: overall (lenient), strict (wontfix counts), objective (mechanical only), verified (confirmed fixes only).
    92→
    93→### Subjective reviews in detail
    94→
    95→- **Preferred**: `desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` — does everything in one command.
    96→- **Manual path**: `desloppify review --prepare` → review per dimension → `desloppify review --import file.json`.
    97→- Import first, fix after — import creates tracked state entries for correlation.
    98→- Integrity: reviewers score from evidence only. Scores hitting exact targets trigger auto-reset.
    99→- Even moderate scores (60-80) dramatically improve overall health.
   100→- Stale dimensions auto-surface in `next` — just follow the queue.
   101→
   102→### Key concepts
   103→
   104→- **Tiers**: T1 auto-fix → T2 quick manual → T3 judgment call → T4 major refactor.
   105→- **Auto-clusters**: related findings are auto-grouped in `next`. Drill in with `next --cluster <name>`.
   106→- **Zones**: production/script (scored), test/config/generated/vendor (not scored). Fix with `zone set`.
   107→- **Epics**: `plan synthesize` groups review findings by root cause into strategic refactoring arcs. Run it when `next` shows "Meta-plan update needed".
   108→- **Wontfix cost**: widens the lenient↔strict gap. Challenge past decisions when the gap grows.
   109→- Score can temporarily drop after fixes (cascade effects are normal).
   110→
   111→## 4. Escalate Tool Issues Upstream
   112→
   113→When desloppify itself appears wrong or inconsistent:
   114→
   115→1. Capture a minimal repro (`command`, `path`, `expected`, `actual`).
   116→2. Open a GitHub issue in `peteromallet/desloppify`.
   117→3. If you can fix it safely, open a PR linked to that issue.
   118→4. If unsure whether it is tool bug vs user workflow, issue first, PR second.
   119→
   120→## Prerequisite
   121→
   122→`command -v desloppify >/dev/null 2>&1 && echo "desloppify: installed" || echo "NOT INSTALLED — run: pip install --upgrade git+https://github.com/peteromallet/desloppify.git"`
   123→
   124→<!-- desloppify-end -->
   125→

   153→def _render_item(
   154→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
   155→    potentials: dict | None = None,

   162→    tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))

   165→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))

   179→        )

   181→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}% (always queued as T4)")

   227→
   228→    target_line = detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
   229→    if target_line and item.get("file") not in (".", ""):
   230→        snippet = read_code_snippet(item["file"], target_line)
   231→        if snippet:

   235→    if dim_scores:
   236→        detector = item.get("detector", "")

   238→        if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   239→            dimension_score = dim_scores[dimension.name]
   240→            strict_val = dimension_score.get("strict", dimension_score["score"])
   241→            print(

   248→            )

   250→    # Score impact estimate
   251→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   252→    if potentials and detector and dim_scores:

   255→            impact = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=1)
   256→            if impact > 0:
   257→                print(colorize(f"  Impact: fixing this is worth ~+{impact:.1f} pts on overall score", "cyan"))
   258→            else:
   259→                # Single-item impact rounds to 0 — try bulk impact for all issues in detector
   260→                dimension = scoring_mod.get_dimension_for_detector(detector)
   261→                if dimension and dimension.name in dim_scores:
   262→                    issues = dim_scores[dimension.name].get("issues", 0)
   263→                    if issues > 1:
   264→                        bulk = compute_score_impact(dim_scores, potentials, detector, issues_to_fix=issues)
   265→                        if bulk > 0:

   268→                                "cyan",
   269→                            ))
   270→        except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError):
   271→            pass
   272→    elif detector == "review" and dim_scores:
   273→        # Review findings: show dimension drag from breakdown
   274→        try:

   276→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")

   278→                breakdown = compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)

   280→                    if not isinstance(entry, dict):

   294→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   295→    auto_fix_command = item.get("primary_command")
   296→    if is_auto_fix_command(auto_fix_command):
   297→        similar_count = sum(

   313→        base = (

   338→
   339→        policy = explanation.get("policy")

   344→
   345→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   346→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   347→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   348→        return
   349→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   350→    if not explain:
   351→        return
   352→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   353→    if available:
   354→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   355→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   356→
   357→
   358→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   359→    if queue.get("items"):
   360→        return False
   361→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   362→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   363→    if tier is not None:
   364→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   365→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   366→        if available:
   367→            commands = " | ".join(
   368→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   369→            )
   370→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   371→    return True
   372→
   373→
   374→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   375→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   376→    if not plan:
   377→        return
   378→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   379→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   380→    if not summary:
   381→        return
   382→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   383→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   384→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   385→
   386→
   387→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   388→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   389→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   390→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   391→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   392→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   393→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   394→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   395→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   396→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   397→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   398→
   399→    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
   400→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   401→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   402→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   403→    print()
   404→    if new:
   405→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   406→    if resolved:
   407→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   408→    if epic_count:
   409→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   410→    print()
   411→    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
   412→    print()
   413→    print("  Steps:")
   414→    print("    1. Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see all review findings")
   415→    print("    2. Analyze findings — group by root cause into clusters")
   416→    print("    3. Create clusters, set priorities, focus on first arc")
   417→    print('    4. Mark complete: `desloppify plan done synthesis::pending --note "..." --attest "..."`')
   418→    print("    5. THEN run `desloppify next` for implementation tasks")
   419→    print()
   420→    print("  Preview:  desloppify plan synthesize --dry-run")
   421→    print()
   422→    print("  View plan:")
   423→    print("    desloppify plan queue              — see all items in priority order")
   424→    print("    desloppify plan show               — plan status summary")
   425→
   426→
   427→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   428→    """Render an epic card."""
   429→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   430→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   431→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   432→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   433→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   434→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   435→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   436→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   437→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   438→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   439→
   440→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   441→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   442→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   443→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   444→    if root_cause:
   445→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   446→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   447→    if total_epics:
   448→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   449→    print()
   450→    if action_steps:
   451→        print("  Steps:")
   452→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   453→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   454→        print()
   455→    if finding_ids:
   456→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   457→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   458→        for fid in shown:
   459→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   460→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   461→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   462→        print()
   463→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   464→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   465→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   466→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   467→
   468→
   469→def render_terminal_items(
   470→    items: list[dict],
   471→    dim_scores: dict,
   472→    findings_scoped: dict,
   473→    *,
   474→    group: str,
   475→    explain: bool,
   476→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   477→    plan: dict | None = None,
   478→) -> None:
   479→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   480→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   481→
   482→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   483→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   484→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   485→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   486→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   487→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   488→        visible = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed")
   489→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({visible} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   490→
   491→        # Exclude the synthesis item from the focus count — it's a queue-level
   492→        # blocker, not a cluster member, so don't count it in "N of M remaining".
   493→        focus_items = [it for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed"]
   494→        if len(focus_items) == 0:
   495→            print(colorize(
   496→                "  This cluster has no remaining items. "
   497→                "Run `desloppify plan focus --clear` to unfocus, "
   498→                "or `desloppify plan cluster show` to inspect.",
   499→                "yellow",
   500→            ))
   501→
   502→    # When synthesis is pending, only show the planning card — don't leak
   503→    # implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip the planning step.
   504→    if items and items[0].get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   505→        queue_pos = items[0].get("queue_position")
   506→        pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   507→        print(colorize(f"  Next item{pos_str}", "bold"))
   508→        _render_synthesis_item(items[0])
   509→        return
   510→
   511→    if group != "item":
   512→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   513→        return
   514→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   515→        if idx > 0:
   516→            print()
   517→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   518→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   519→
   520→        # All item types get the same position label — no visual hierarchy
   521→        # that makes synthetic items look like metadata instead of tasks.
   522→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   523→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   524→        elif len(items) > 1:
   525→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   526→        else:
   527→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   528→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   529→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   530→
   531→        if kind == "epic":
   532→            _render_epic_item(item)
   533→            continue
   534→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   535→
   536→
   537→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   538→    if len(items) != 1:
   539→        return
   540→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   541→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   542→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   543→    if kind != "finding":
   544→        return
   545→    item = items[0]
   546→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   547→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   548→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   549→        primary = item.get(
   550→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   551→        )
   552→        print(f"    {primary}")
   553→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   554→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   555→        return
   556→
   557→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   558→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   559→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   560→        print(f"    {primary}")
   561→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   562→    else:
   563→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   564→
   565→    print(
   566→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   567→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   568→    )
   569→    print(
   570→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   571→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   572→    )
   573→
   574→
   575→def render_followup_nudges(
   576→    state: dict,
   577→    dim_scores: dict,
   578→    findings_scoped: dict,
   579→    *,
   580→    strict_score: float | None,
   581→    target_strict_score: float,
   582→) -> None:
   583→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   584→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   585→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   586→        state,
   587→        subjective_entries,
   588→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   589→        max_quality_items=3,
   590→        max_integrity_items=5,
   591→    )
   592→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   593→    if strict_score is not None:
   594→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   595→        if gap > 0:
   596→            print(
   597→                colorize(
   598→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   599→                    "cyan",
   600→                )
   601→            )
   602→        else:
   603→            print(
   604→                colorize(
   605→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   606→                    "green",
   607→                )
   608→            )
   609→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   610→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   611→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   612→
   613→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   614→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   615→        findings_scoped
   616→    )
   617→    parts: list[str] = []
   618→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   619→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   620→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   621→    open_review = [
   622→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   623→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   624→    ]
   625→    if low_dims:
   626→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   627→    if stale_count:
   628→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   629→    if unassessed_count:
   630→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   631→    if len(open_review):
   632→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   633→    if coverage_open > 0:
   634→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   635→
   636→    if parts:
   637→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   638→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   639→
   640→
   641→__all__ = [
   642→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   643→    "render_followup_nudges",
   644→    "render_queue_header",
   645→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   646→    "render_terminal_items",
   647→    "scorecard_subjective",
   648→    "show_empty_queue",
   649→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   650→]
   651→

    11→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    18→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    19→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    20→    plan = load_plan()
    21→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    22→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    23→    total_skipped = len(skipped)

    25→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    26→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    27→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    28→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    29→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    30→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    31→
    32→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    33→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    34→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    35→
    36→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    37→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    38→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    39→    if total_skipped:

    41→    else:

    43→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    44→    if clusters:

    46→        auto = {n: c for n, c in clusters.items() if c.get("auto")}
    47→        if manual:
    48→            for name, cluster in manual.items():
    49→                desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    50→                member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    51→                marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""

    53→                print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")

    60→    if active:
    61→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    62→    if superseded:
    63→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    64→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
    65→    if epics:
    66→        print(f"  Epics:            {len(epics)}")
    67→        for name, epic in epics.items():
    68→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
    69→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
    70→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
    71→            fcount = len(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
    72→            safe_tag = " [agent-safe]" if epic.get("agent_safe") else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name} [{direction}] ({fcount} findings, {status}){safe_tag}")
    74→            if thesis:
    75→                print(f"      {thesis}")
    76→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    77→    if meta and meta.get("strategy_summary"):
    78→        print(f"  Strategy:         {meta['strategy_summary']}")
    79→        print(f"  Last synthesis:   v{meta.get('version', 0)} ({meta.get('trigger', 'unknown')} @ {meta.get('last_run', 'never')})")
    80→
    81→
    82→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    83→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    84→    from desloppify.engine.plan import reset_plan
    85→
    86→    plan = load_plan()
    87→    reset_plan(plan)
    88→    save_plan(plan)
    89→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    90→
    91→
    92→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    93→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    94→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    95→
    96→    if plan_action is None:
    97→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if plan_action == "show":
   101→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "queue":
   105→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.queue_render import cmd_plan_queue
   106→
   107→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   108→        return
   109→
   110→    if plan_action == "reset":
   111→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   112→        return
   113→
   114→    if plan_action == "move":
   115→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.move_handlers import cmd_plan_move
   116→
   117→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    if plan_action in (
   121→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   122→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   123→    ):
   124→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.override_handlers import (
   125→            cmd_plan_describe,
   126→            cmd_plan_done,
   127→            cmd_plan_focus,
   128→            cmd_plan_note,
   129→            cmd_plan_reopen,
   130→            cmd_plan_skip,
   131→            cmd_plan_unskip,
   132→        )
   133→
   134→        dispatch = {
   135→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   136→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   137→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   138→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   139→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   140→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   141→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   142→        }
   143→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   147→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cluster_handlers import cmd_cluster_dispatch
   148→
   149→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   153→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   154→
   155→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   159→
   160→
   161→# Backwards-compatible alias
   162→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   163→
   164→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   165→

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    33→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
    34→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    35→    state = runtime.state
    36→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    40→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    41→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    42→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    43→
    44→    plan = load_plan()
    45→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    46→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    47→        plan_data = plan
    48→
    49→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster
    50→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    51→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    52→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    53→        if active_cluster:
    54→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    55→
    56→    queue = build_work_queue(
    57→        state,
    58→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    59→            tier=tier_filter,
    60→            count=None,
    61→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
    62→            status="open",
    63→            include_subjective=True,
    64→            plan=plan_data,
    65→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
    66→            cluster=effective_cluster,
    67→            collapse_clusters=True,
    68→        ),
    69→    )
    70→    items = queue.get("items", [])
    71→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
    72→
    73→    # Header
    74→    total = len(items)
    75→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    76→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    77→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    78→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    79→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    80→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    81→    else:
    82→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    83→
    84→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    85→    if focus:
    86→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    87→
    88→    if not include_skipped and skipped_count == 0:
    89→        # Check plan for skipped items not in queue
    90→        plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    91→        if plan_skipped_count:
    92→            print(colorize(
    93→                f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    94→                " hidden \u2014 use --include-skipped)",
    95→                "dim",
    96→            ))
    97→
    98→    if not items:
    99→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   100→        return
   101→
   102→    # Determine which items to show
   103→    display_items = items
   104→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   105→        display_items = items[:top]
   106→
   107→    # Build rows
   108→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   109→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
   110→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
   111→        pos = str(idx)
   112→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   113→
   114→        if kind == "synthesis_needed":
   115→            tier_str = "T1"
   116→            detector = "synthesis"
   117→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   118→            n = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   119→            summary = f"[synthesis] {n} findings need integration"
   120→            cluster_name = ""
   121→        elif kind == "epic":
   122→            tier_str = "T1"
   123→            detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   124→            direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   125→            fcount = len(detail.get("finding_ids", []))
   126→            status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   127→            detector = "epic"
   128→            summary = f"[{direction}] [{fcount}] [{status}] {detail.get('thesis', '')}"
   129→            cluster_name = item.get("id", "")
   130→        elif kind == "cluster":
   131→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
   132→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   133→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   134→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   135→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   136→        else:
   137→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   138→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   139→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   140→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   141→            # Extract 8-char hash suffix so users can reference items by short ID
   142→            item_id = item.get("id", "")
   143→            if "::" in item_id:
   144→                hash_suffix = item_id.rsplit("::", 1)[-1][:8]
   145→                # Truncate summary first, then append hash so it's never cut off
   146→                summary = f"{_truncate(summary, 36)} [{hash_suffix}]"
   147→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   148→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   149→
   150→        suffix = ""
   151→        if item.get("plan_skipped"):
   152→            suffix = " [skip]"
   153→
   154→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   155→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   156→
   157→    print()
   158→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 44, 24]
   159→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   160→
   161→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   162→        remaining = len(items) - top
   163→        print(colorize(
   164→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   165→        ))
   166→    print()
   167→
   168→
   169→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   170→
