```
1→"""LLM-facing reporting helpers for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import os
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify import scoring as scoring_mod
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_text import build_workflow_guide
    12→from desloppify.engine.planning import scorecard_projection as scorecard_projection_mod
    13→from desloppify.core import registry as registry_mod
    14→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    15→from desloppify.core import skill_docs as skill_docs_mod
    16→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    17→
    18→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    19→
    20→
    21→def _is_agent_environment() -> bool:
    22→    return bool(
    23→        os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE")
    24→        or os.environ.get("DESLOPPIFY_AGENT")
    25→        or os.environ.get("GEMINI_CLI")
    26→        or os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX_NETWORK_DISABLED")
    27→        or os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX")
    28→        or os.environ.get("CURSOR_TRACE_ID")
    29→    )
    30→
    31→
    32→def _load_scores(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
    33→    """Load all four canonical scores from state."""
    34→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _print_score_lines(
    38→    *,
    39→    overall_score: float | None,
    40→    objective_score: float | None,
    41→    strict_score: float | None,
    42→    verified_score: float | None,
    43→) -> None:
    44→    lines: list[str] = []
    45→    if overall_score is not None:
    46→        lines.append(f"Overall score:   {overall_score:.1f}/100")
    47→    if objective_score is not None:
    48→        lines.append(f"Objective score: {objective_score:.1f}/100")
    49→    if strict_score is not None:
    50→        lines.append(f"Strict score:    {strict_score:.1f}/100")
    51→    if verified_score is not None:
    52→        lines.append(f"Verified score:  {verified_score:.1f}/100")
    53→    if lines:
    54→        print("\n".join(lines))
    55→    # Score legend — always shown in LLM block so agents understand the scoring model
    56→    print("Score guide:")
    57→    print("  overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)")
    58→    print("  objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)")
    59→    print("  strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star")
    60→    print("  verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes")
    61→    print()
    62→
    63→
    64→def _split_dimension_scores(
    65→    state: dict,
    66→    dim_scores: dict,
    67→) -> tuple[list[tuple[str, dict]], list[tuple[str, dict]]]:
    68→    # Build dimension table from canonical scorecard projection.
    69→    rows = scorecard_projection_mod.scorecard_dimension_rows(
    70→        state, dim_scores=dim_scores
    71→    )
    72→    subjective_name_set = {name.lower() for name in scoring_mod.DISPLAY_NAMES.values()}
    73→    subjective_name_set.update({"elegance", "elegance (combined)"})
    74→
    75→    mechanical = [
    76→        (name, data)
    77→        for name, data in rows
    78→        if (
    79→            "subjective_assessment" not in data.get("detectors", {})
    80→            and str(name).strip().lower() not in subjective_name_set
    81→        )
    82→    ]
    83→    subjective = [
    84→        (name, data)
    85→        for name, data in rows
    86→        if (
    87→            "subjective_assessment" in data.get("detectors", {})
    88→            or str(name).strip().lower() in subjective_name_set
    89→        )
    90→    ]
    91→    return mechanical, subjective
    92→
    93→
    94→def _print_dimension_table(state: dict, dim_scores: dict) -> None:
    95→    mechanical, subjective = _split_dimension_scores(state, dim_scores)
    96→    if not (mechanical or subjective):
    97→        return
    98→
    99→    print("| Dimension | Health | Strict | Issues | Tier | Action |")
   100→    print("|-----------|--------|--------|--------|------|--------|")
   101→    for name, data in sorted(mechanical, key=lambda item: item[0]):
   102→        score = data.get("score", 100)
   103→        strict = data.get("strict", score)
   104→        issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   105→        tier = data.get("tier", "")
   106→        action = registry_mod.dimension_action_type(name)
   107→        print(
   108→            f"| {name} | {score:.1f}% | {strict:.1f}% | {issues} | T{tier} | {action} |"
   109→        )
   110→    if subjective:
   111→        print("| **Subjective Dimensions** | | | | | |")
   112→        for name, data in sorted(subjective, key=lambda item: item[0]):
   113→            score = data.get("score", 100)
   114→            strict = data.get("strict", score)
   115→            issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   116→            tier = data.get("tier", "")
   117→            print(
   118→                f"| {name} | {score:.1f}% | {strict:.1f}% | {issues} | T{tier} | review |"
   119→            )
   120→    print()
   121→
   122→
   123→def _print_drag_summary(dim_scores: dict) -> None:
   124→    """Print the biggest score-drag dimensions so agents know where to focus."""
   125→    if not dim_scores:
   126→        return
   127→    try:
   128→        breakdown = scoring_mod.compute_health_breakdown(dim_scores)
   129→        entries = breakdown.get("entries", [])
   130→        drags = sorted(
   131→            [e for e in entries if isinstance(e, dict) and float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0) > 0.01],
   132→            key=lambda e: -float(e.get("overall_drag", 0) or 0),
   133→        )
   134→        if drags:
   135→            print("Biggest score drags (fixing these dimensions has the most impact):")
   136→            for entry in drags[:5]:
   137→                print(
   138→                    f"  - {entry['name']}: -{float(entry['overall_drag']):.2f} pts "
   139→                    f"(score {float(entry['score']):.1f}%, "
   140→                    f"{float(entry['pool_share'])*100:.1f}% of {entry['pool']} pool)"
   141→                )
   142→            print()
   143→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError, KeyError) as exc:
   144→        logger.debug("Drag summary skipped: %s", exc)
   145→
   146→
   147→def _print_stats_summary(
   148→    state: dict,
   149→    diff: dict | None,
   150→    *,
   151→    overall_score: float | None,
   152→    strict_score: float | None,
   153→) -> None:
   154→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   155→    if not stats:
   156→        return
   157→
   158→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   159→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0) if diff else 0
   160→    ignore_pats = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0) if diff else 0
   161→    strict_gap = (
   162→        round((overall_score or 0) - (strict_score or 0), 1)
   163→        if overall_score and strict_score
   164→        else 0
   165→    )
   166→    print(
   167→        f"Total findings: {stats.get('total', 0)} | "
   168→        f"Open: {stats.get('open', 0)} | "
   169→        f"Fixed: {stats.get('fixed', 0)} | "
   170→        f"Wontfix: {wontfix}"
   171→    )
   172→    if wontfix or ignored or ignore_pats:
   173→        print(
   174→            f"Ignored: {ignored} (by {ignore_pats} patterns) | Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   175→        )
   176→        print("Focus on strict score — wontfix and ignore inflate the lenient score.")
   177→    print()
   178→
   179→
   180→_WORKFLOW_GUIDE = build_workflow_guide(ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   181→
   182→
   183→def _print_workflow_guide() -> None:
   184→    # Workflow guide — teach agents the full cycle
   185→    print(_WORKFLOW_GUIDE)
   186→    print()
   187→
   188→
   189→def _print_narrative_status(narrative: dict | None) -> None:
   190→    if not narrative:
   191→        return
   192→
   193→    headline = narrative.get("headline", "")
   194→    strategy = narrative.get("strategy") or {}
   195→    actions = narrative.get("actions", [])
   196→    if headline:
   197→        print(f"Current status: {headline}")
   198→    hint = strategy.get("hint", "")
   199→    if hint:
   200→        print(f"Strategy: {hint}")
   201→    if actions:
   202→        top = actions[0]
   203→        print(f"Top action: `{top['command']}` — {top['description']}")
   204→    print()
   205→
   206→
   207→def _detect_agent_interface() -> str | None:
   208→    """Detect the current agent interface from environment variables."""
   209→    if os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CODE"):
   210→        return "claude"
   211→    if os.environ.get("GEMINI_CLI"):
   212→        return "gemini"
   213→    if os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX_NETWORK_DISABLED") or os.environ.get("CODEX_SANDBOX"):
   214→        return "codex"
   215→    if os.environ.get("CURSOR_TRACE_ID"):
   216→        return "cursor"
   217→    return None
   218→
   219→
   220→def _try_auto_update_skill() -> None:
   221→    """Attempt to auto-install or auto-update the skill document.
   222→
   223→    Best-effort: swallows all exceptions so a network failure or permission
   224→    error never breaks the scan.
   225→    """
   226→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
   227→
   228→    if install and not install.stale:
   229→        return  # Up to date.
   230→
   231→    try:
   232→        from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import (
   233→            resolve_interface,
   234→            update_installed_skill,
   235→        )
   236→
   237→        if install:
   238→            interface = resolve_interface(install=install)
   239→        else:
   240→            interface = _detect_agent_interface()
   241→
   242→        if interface:
   243→            update_installed_skill(interface)
   244→    except (ImportError, OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError) as exc:
   245→        logger.debug("Skill auto-update skipped: %s", exc, exc_info=True)
   246→
   247→
   248→def _print_badge_hint(badge_path: Path | None) -> None:
   249→    if not (badge_path and badge_path.exists()):
   250→        return
   251→
   252→    rel_path = badge_path.name if badge_path.parent == PROJECT_ROOT else str(badge_path)
   253→    print(f"A scorecard image was saved to `{rel_path}`.")
   254→    print("Let the user know they can view it, and suggest adding it")
   255→    print(f'to their README: `<img src="{rel_path}" width="100%">`')
   256→
   257→
   258→def _print_llm_summary(
   259→    state: dict,
   260→    badge_path: Path | None,
   261→    narrative: dict | None = None,
   262→    diff: dict | None = None,
   263→):
   264→    """Print a structured summary for LLM consumption.
   265→
   266→    The LLM reads terminal output after running scans. This gives it
   267→    clear instructions on how to present the results to the end user.
   268→    Only shown when running inside an agent (CLAUDE_CODE or DESLOPPIFY_AGENT env).
   269→    """
   270→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   274→    scores = _load_scores(state)
   275→
   276→    if (
   277→        scores.overall is None
   278→        and scores.objective is None
   279→        and scores.strict is None
   280→        and scores.verified is None
   281→        and not dim_scores
   282→    ):
   283→        return
   284→
   285→    print("─" * 60)
   286→    print("INSTRUCTIONS FOR LLM")
   287→    print("IMPORTANT: ALWAYS present ALL scores to the user after a scan.")
   288→    print("Show overall health (lenient + strict), ALL dimension scores,")
   289→    print("AND all subjective dimension scores in a markdown table.")
   290→    print("The goal is to maximize strict scores. Never skip the scores.\n")
   291→
   292→    # Check for living plan
   293→    try:
   294→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   295→        _plan = load_plan()
   296→        _has_plan = bool(
   297→            _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")
   298→            or _plan.get("skipped")
   299→        )
   300→    except Exception:
   301→        _plan = {}
   302→        _has_plan = False
   303→
   304→    if _has_plan:
   305→        ordered = len(_plan.get("queue_order", []))
   306→        skipped = len(_plan.get("skipped", {}))
   307→        active = _plan.get("active_cluster")
   308→        print(f"LIVING PLAN ACTIVE: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.")
   309→        if active:
   310→            cluster = _plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
   311→            remaining = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   312→            print(f"Focused on: {active} ({remaining} items remaining).")
   313→        epics = _plan.get("epics", {})
   314→        if epics:
   315→            meta = _plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   316→            strategy = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   317→            pending = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") != "completed")
   318→            print(f"Epics: {len(epics)} ({pending} active).")
   319→            if strategy:
   320→                print(f"Strategy: {strategy}")
   321→        print("The plan is the single source of truth for work order.")
   322→        print("Use `desloppify next` which respects the plan.")
   323→        print("Use `desloppify plan` to view and update it.\n")
   324→
   325→    _print_score_lines(
   326→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   327→        objective_score=scores.objective,
   328→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   329→        verified_score=scores.verified,
   330→    )
   331→    _print_dimension_table(state, dim_scores)
   332→    _print_drag_summary(dim_scores)
   333→    _print_stats_summary(
   334→        state,
   335→        diff,
   336→        overall_score=scores.overall,
   337→        strict_score=scores.strict,
   338→    )
   339→    if _has_plan:
   340→        print("\nFollow the living plan: `desloppify next` for your next task,")
   341→        print("`desloppify plan` to view the full queue.")
   342→    else:
   343→        _print_workflow_guide()
   344→    _print_narrative_status(narrative)
   345→    _print_badge_hint(badge_path)
   346→    print("─" * 60)
   347→
   348→
   349→def auto_update_skill() -> None:
   350→    """Auto-install or update the skill document if we detect an agent.
   351→
   352→    Called unconditionally from the scan workflow — not gated on scores.
   353→    """
   354→    if not _is_agent_environment():
   355→        return
   356→
   357→    _try_auto_update_skill()
   358→
   359→    # Single post-check: whatever happened above, is the doc current now?
   360→    install = skill_docs_mod.find_installed_skill()
   361→    if not install:

   363→        print(
```

```
   194→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

   208→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
   209→    _save_config_or_exit(config)

   214→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   215→            "command": "ignore",
   216→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   217→            "attestation": attestation,

   220→    )
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
     9→    ALL_STATUSES,
    10→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    11→    build_subjective_items,
    12→    build_synthesis_item,
    13→    scope_matches,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    16→    build_finding_items,
    17→    choose_fallback_tier,
    18→    group_queue_items,
    19→    item_explain,
    20→    item_sort_key,
    21→    tier_counts,
    22→)
    23→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    24→
    25→
    26→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    27→class QueueBuildOptions:
    28→    """Configuration for queue construction and tier selection behavior."""
    29→
    30→    tier: int | None = None
    31→    count: int | None = 1
    32→    scan_path: str | None = None
    33→    scope: str | None = None
    34→    status: str = "open"
    35→    include_subjective: bool = True
    36→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    37→    chronic: bool = False
    38→    no_tier_fallback: bool = False
    39→    explain: bool = False
    40→    plan: dict | None = None
    41→    include_skipped: bool = False
    42→    cluster: str | None = None
    43→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    44→
    45→
    46→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    47→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    48→
    49→    items: list[dict]
    50→    total: int
    51→    tier_counts: dict[int, int]
    52→    requested_tier: int | None
    53→    selected_tier: int | None
    54→    fallback_reason: str | None
    55→    available_tiers: list[int]
    56→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    57→
    58→
    59→def _apply_plan_order(
    60→    items: list[dict],
    61→    plan: dict,
    62→    *,
    63→    include_skipped: bool = False,
    64→    cluster: str | None = None,
    65→    state: dict | None = None,
    66→) -> list[dict]:
    67→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
    68→
    69→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
    70→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
    71→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
    72→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
    73→    """
    74→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    75→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    76→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
    77→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
    78→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
    79→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
    80→
    81→    # Build lookup
    82→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
    83→    for item in items:
    84→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
    85→
    86→    # Build synthetic items for skipped entries missing from the work queue
    87→    if include_skipped and state:
    88→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    89→        for fid in skipped_ids:
    90→            if fid not in by_id and fid in findings:
    91→                f = findings[fid]
    92→                by_id[fid] = {
    93→                    "id": fid,
    94→                    "kind": "finding",
    95→                    "tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    96→                    "effective_tier": f.get("tier", 3),
    97→                    "confidence": f.get("confidence", "medium"),
    98→                    "detector": f.get("detector", ""),
    99→                    "file": f.get("file", ""),
   100→                    "summary": f.get("summary", ""),
   101→                    "status": f.get("status", "open"),
   102→                }
   103→
   104→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   105→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   106→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   107→        if override.get("description"):
   108→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   109→        if override.get("note"):
   110→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   111→        if override.get("cluster"):
   112→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   113→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   114→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   115→                "name": cluster_name,
   116→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   117→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   118→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   119→            }
   120→
   121→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   122→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   123→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   124→    for fid in queue_order:
   125→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   126→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   127→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   128→
   129→    skipped_items: list[dict] = []
   130→    remaining: list[dict] = []
   131→    for item in items:
   132→        item_id = item["id"]
   133→        if item_id in ordered_ids:
   134→            continue
   135→        if item_id in skipped_ids:
   136→            skipped_items.append(item)
   137→        else:
   138→            remaining.append(item)
   139→
   140→    # Assign queue positions
   141→    result = ordered + remaining
   142→    if include_skipped:
   143→        result = result + skipped_items
   144→
   145→    for pos, item in enumerate(result):
   146→        item["queue_position"] = pos + 1
   147→        if item["id"] in skipped_ids:
   148→            item["plan_skipped"] = True
   149→            skip_entry = skipped_map.get(item["id"])
   150→            if skip_entry:
   151→                item["plan_skip_kind"] = skip_entry.get("kind", "temporary")
   152→                skip_reason = skip_entry.get("reason")
   153→                if skip_reason:
   154→                    item["plan_skip_reason"] = skip_reason
   155→
   156→    # Filter to cluster if requested — but never filter out synthesis items;
   157→    # they are queue-level blockers that outrank cluster membership.
   158→    effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
   159→    if effective_cluster:
   160→        cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
   161→        cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   162→        result = [
   163→            item for item in result
   164→            if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids
   165→            or item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed"
   166→        ]
   167→
   168→    return result
   169→
   170→
   171→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   172→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   173→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
   174→    if kind in ("synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   175→        return tier == 1  # Always T1
   176→    if kind == "cluster":
   177→        return any(
   178→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   179→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   180→        )
   181→    return int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   182→
   183→
   184→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   185→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   186→    try:
   187→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   188→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   189→        if meta:
   190→            return meta.action_type
   191→    except ImportError:
   192→        pass
   193→    return "manual_fix"
   194→
   195→
   196→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   197→
   198→
   199→def _epic_membership(epics: dict) -> dict[str, str]:
   200→    """Build mapping of finding id to epic name."""
   201→    finding_to_epic: dict[str, str] = {}
   202→    for epic_name, epic in epics.items():
   203→        if epic.get("status") == "completed":
   204→            continue
   205→        for finding_id in epic.get("finding_ids", []):
   206→            finding_to_epic[finding_id] = epic_name
   207→    return finding_to_epic
   208→
   209→
   210→def _build_epic_items(plan: dict, state: StateModel) -> list[dict]:
   211→    """Build synthetic queue items for each pending/in_progress epic."""
   212→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   213→    if not epics:
   214→        return []
   215→
   216→    total_epics = len(epics)
   217→    items: list[dict] = []
   218→    for epic_name, epic in sorted(
   219→        epics.items(),
   220→        key=lambda kv: kv[1].get("dependency_order", 999),
   221→    ):
   222→        status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   223→        if status == "completed":
   224→            continue
   225→        finding_ids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   226→        if not finding_ids:
   227→            continue
   228→
   229→        items.append({
   230→            "id": epic_name,
   231→            "kind": "epic",
   232→            "tier": 1,
   233→            "effective_tier": 1,
   234→            "confidence": "high",
   235→            "detector": "epic",
   236→            "file": ".",
   237→            "summary": f"[{epic.get('direction', 'simplify')}] {epic.get('thesis', '')}",
   238→            "status": "open",
   239→            "primary_command": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   240→            "detail": {
   241→                "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   242→                "direction": epic.get("direction", ""),
   243→                "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   244→                "epic_status": status,
   245→                "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   246→                "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   247→                "total_epics": total_epics,
   248→                "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   249→                "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   250→            },
   251→        })
   252→    return items
   253→
   254→
   255→def _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   256→    *,
   257→    all_items: list[dict],
   258→    plan: dict,
   259→    state: StateModel,
   260→    scope: str | None,
   261→) -> None:
   262→    """Inject synthesis::pending and epic items into the queue."""
   263→    existing_ids = {item.get("id") for item in all_items}
   264→
   265→    synthesis_item = build_synthesis_item(plan, state)
   266→    if synthesis_item and synthesis_item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   267→        if scope_matches(synthesis_item, scope):
   268→            all_items.append(synthesis_item)
   269→            existing_ids.add(synthesis_item["id"])
   270→
   271→    epic_items = _build_epic_items(plan, state)
   272→    for item in epic_items:
   273→        if item["id"] not in existing_ids:
   274→            if scope_matches(item, scope):
   275→                all_items.append(item)
   276→                existing_ids.add(item["id"])
   277→
   278→
   279→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   280→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items.
   281→
   282→    Epic membership takes precedence: findings in epics are excluded
   283→    from cluster collapsing (they'll be represented by epic items).
   284→    """
   285→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   286→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   287→
   288→    # Build epic membership to exclude from cluster collapse
   289→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   290→    if epics:
   291→        for epic in epics.values():
   292→            if epic.get("status") != "completed":
   293→                epic_finding_ids.update(epic.get("finding_ids", []))
   294→
   295→    if not clusters:
   296→        return items
   297→
   298→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   299→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   300→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   301→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   302→            continue
   303→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   304→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   305→
   306→    # Remove epic findings from cluster membership
   307→    for fid in epic_finding_ids:
   308→        fid_to_cluster.pop(fid, None)
   309→
   310→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   311→        return items
   312→
   313→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   314→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   315→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   316→
   317→    for item in items:
   318→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   319→        if cname:
   320→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   321→        else:
   322→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   323→
   324→    # Build cluster meta-items
   325→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   326→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   327→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   328→        if len(members) < 2:
   329→            result.extend(members)
   330→            continue
   331→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   332→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   333→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   334→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   335→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   336→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   337→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   338→            action_type = "reorganize"
   339→        else:
   340→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   341→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   342→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   343→                action_type = "refactor"
   344→
   345→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   346→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   347→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   348→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   349→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   350→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   351→        else:
   352→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   353→
   354→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   355→        if not primary_command:
   356→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   357→
   358→        meta_item: dict = {
   359→            "id": cname,
   360→            "kind": "cluster",
   361→            "action_type": action_type,
   362→            "summary": summary,
   363→            "members": members,
   364→            "member_count": len(members),
   365→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   366→            "cluster_name": cname,
   367→            "cluster_auto": True,
   368→            "confidence": "high",
   369→            "detector": detector,
   370→            "file": "",
   371→        }
   372→        result.append(meta_item)
   373→
   374→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   375→    return result
   376→
   377→
   378→def build_work_queue(
   379→    state: StateModel,
   380→    *,
   381→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   382→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   383→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   384→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   385→
   386→    status = resolved_options.status
   387→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   388→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   389→    try:
   390→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   391→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   392→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   393→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   394→
   395→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   396→        state,
   397→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   398→        status_filter=status,
   399→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   400→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   401→    )
   402→
   403→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   404→    if (
   405→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   406→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   407→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   408→    ):
   409→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   410→            state,
   411→            state.get("findings", {}),
   412→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   413→        )
   414→        for item in subjective_items:
   415→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   416→                all_items.append(item)
   417→
   418→    # Inject synthesis::pending and epic items when plan has them
   419→    if resolved_options.plan:
   420→        _inject_synthesis_and_epic_items(
   421→            all_items=all_items,
   422→            plan=resolved_options.plan,
   423→            state=state,
   424→            scope=resolved_options.scope,
   425→        )
   426→
   427→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   428→
   429→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   430→    if resolved_options.plan:
   431→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   432→            all_items,
   433→            resolved_options.plan,
   434→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   435→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   436→            state=state if resolved_options.include_skipped else None,
   437→        )
   438→
   439→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   440→    should_collapse = (
   441→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   442→        and resolved_options.plan
   443→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   444→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   445→    )
   446→    if should_collapse:
   447→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   448→
   449→    counts = tier_counts(all_items)
   450→
   451→    requested_tier = (
   452→        int(resolved_options.tier) if resolved_options.tier is not None else None
   453→    )
   454→    selected_tier = requested_tier
   455→    fallback_reason = None
   456→    filtered = all_items
   457→
   458→    if requested_tier is not None:
   459→        filtered = [
   460→            item
   461→            for item in all_items
   462→            if _item_matches_tier(item, requested_tier)
   463→        ]
   464→        if not filtered and not resolved_options.no_tier_fallback:
   465→            chosen = choose_fallback_tier(requested_tier, counts)
   466→            if chosen is not None:
   467→                selected_tier = chosen
   468→                filtered = [
   469→                    item
   470→                    for item in all_items
   471→                    if _item_matches_tier(item, chosen)
   472→                ]
   473→                fallback_reason = (
   474→                    f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open -> showing T{chosen} "
   475→                    "(nearest non-empty)."
   476→                )
   477→        elif not filtered:
   478→            fallback_reason = f"Requested T{requested_tier} has 0 open."
   479→
   480→    total = len(filtered)
   481→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   482→        filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]
   483→
   484→    if resolved_options.explain:
   485→        for item in filtered:
   486→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   487→
   488→    available_tiers = [tier for tier, value in counts.items() if value > 0]
   489→    return {
   490→        "items": filtered,
   491→        "total": total,
   492→        "tier_counts": counts,
   493→        "requested_tier": requested_tier,
   494→        "selected_tier": selected_tier,
   495→        "fallback_reason": fallback_reason,
   496→        "available_tiers": available_tiers,
   497→        "grouped": group_queue_items(filtered, "item"),
   498→    }
   499→
   500→
   501→__all__ = [
   502→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   503→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   504→    "WorkQueueResult",
   505→    "build_work_queue",
   506→    "group_queue_items",
   507→]
   508→
```

```
    26→def detail_dict(item: dict) -> dict:
    27→    """Return finding detail as a dict; tolerate legacy/non-dict payloads."""
    28→    detail = item.get("detail")
    29→    return detail if isinstance(detail, dict) else {}

    32→def status_matches(item_status: str, status_filter: str) -> bool:

    36→def is_subjective_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    37→    detector = item.get("detector")
    38→    if detector in {"subjective_assessment"}:
    39→        return True
    40→    if detector == "holistic_review":
    41→        return True
    42→    return False
    43→

    45→def is_review_finding(item: dict) -> bool:
    46→    return item.get("detector") == "review"

    49→def review_finding_weight(item: dict) -> float:
    50→    """Return review issue weight aligned with issues list ordering."""
    51→    confidence = str(item.get("confidence", "low")).lower()
    52→    weight_by_confidence = {
    53→        "high": 1.0,
    54→        "medium": 0.7,
    55→        "low": 0.3,
    56→    }
    57→    weight = weight_by_confidence.get(confidence, 0.3)
    58→    if detail_dict(item).get("holistic"):
    59→        weight *= 10.0
    60→    return float(weight)
    61→
    62→
    63→def scope_matches(item: dict, scope: str | None) -> bool:
    64→    """Apply show-style pattern matching against a queue item."""
    65→    if not scope:
    66→        return True
    67→
    68→    item_id = item.get("id", "")
    69→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
    70→    filepath = item.get("file", "")
    71→    summary = item.get("summary", "")
    72→    dimension = detail_dict(item).get("dimension_name", "")
    73→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    74→
    75→    if "*" in scope:
    76→        return any(
    77→            fnmatch(candidate, scope)
    78→            for candidate in (item_id, filepath, detector, dimension, summary)
    79→        )
    80→
    81→    if "::" in scope:
    82→        return item_id.startswith(scope)
    83→
    84→    lowered = scope.lower()
    85→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    86→        return (
    87→            lowered in item_id.lower()
    88→            or lowered in dimension.lower()
    89→            or lowered in summary.lower()
    90→        )
    91→
    92→    # Hash suffix: 8+ hex chars matches the tail segment of a finding ID.
    93→    if len(lowered) >= 8 and re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]+", lowered):
    94→        return item_id.lower().endswith("::" + lowered)
    95→
    96→    return (
    97→        detector == scope
    98→        or filepath == scope
    99→        or filepath.startswith(scope.rstrip("/") + "/")
   100→    )

   103→def slugify(text: str) -> str:
   104→    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9_]+", "_", text.lower()).strip("_")

   107→def _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(display_name: str) -> str:
   108→    """Map a display label (e.g. 'Mid elegance') to its canonical dimension key."""
   109→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   110→    target = cleaned.lower()
   111→
   112→    for dim_key, label in DISPLAY_NAMES.items():
   113→        if str(label).lower() == target:
   114→            return str(dim_key)
   115→    return slugify(cleaned)

   120→    cleaned = display_name.replace(" (subjective)", "").strip()
   121→    canonical = _canonical_subjective_dimension_key(cleaned)
   122→    return {
   123→        cleaned.lower(),
   124→        cleaned.replace(" ", "_").lower(),
   125→        slugify(cleaned),
   126→        canonical.lower(),
   127→        slugify(canonical),
   128→    }

   131→def supported_fixers_for_item(state: dict, item: dict) -> set[str] | None:
   132→    """Return supported fixers for an item's language when known."""
   133→    lang = str(item.get("lang", "") or "").strip()
   134→    if not lang:
   135→        return None
   136→
   137→    caps = state.get("lang_capabilities", {})
   138→    if not isinstance(caps, dict):
   139→        return None
   140→
   141→    lang_caps = caps.get(lang, {})
   142→    if not isinstance(lang_caps, dict):
   143→        return None
   144→
   145→    fixers = lang_caps.get("fixers")
   146→    if not isinstance(fixers, list):
   147→        return None
   148→    return {fixer for fixer in fixers if isinstance(fixer, str)}

   151→def primary_command_for_finding(
   152→    item: dict, *, supported_fixers: set[str] | None = None
   153→) -> str:
   154→    detector = item.get("detector", "")
   155→    meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   156→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers:
   157→        available_fixers = [
   158→            fixer
   159→            for fixer in meta.fixers
   160→            if supported_fixers is None or fixer in supported_fixers
   161→        ]
   162→        if available_fixers:
   163→            return f"desloppify fix {available_fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   164→    if detector == "subjective_review":

   167→        return "desloppify show subjective"

   182→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   183→        [REDACTED](name)
   184→        aliases = _subjective_dimension_aliases(name)
   185→        for cli_key in entry.get("cli_keys", []):
   186→            key = str(cli_key).strip().lower()
   187→            if not key:
   188→                continue
   189→            aliases.add(key)
   190→            aliases.add(slugify(key))
   191→        aliases.add(dim_key.lower())
   192→        aliases.add(slugify(dim_key))
   193→        for alias in aliases:
   194→            scores[alias] = strict_val
   195→    return scores
   196→
   197→
   198→def build_subjective_items(
   199→    state: dict, findings: dict, *, threshold: float = 100.0
   200→) -> list[dict]:
   201→    """Create synthetic subjective work items (always tier 4)."""
   202→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   203→    if not dim_scores:
   204→        return []
   205→    threshold = max(0.0, min(100.0, float(threshold)))
   206→
   207→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores)
   208→    if not subjective_entries:
   209→        return []
   210→    unassessed_dims = {
   211→        str(name).strip()
   212→        for name in unassessed_subjective_dimensions(
   213→            dim_scores
   214→        )
   215→    }
   216→
   217→    # Review findings are keyed by raw dimension name (snake_case).
   218→    review_open_by_dim: dict[str, int] = {}
   219→    for finding in findings.values():
   220→        if finding.get("status") != "open" or finding.get("detector") != "review":
   221→            continue
   222→        dim_key = str(detail_dict(finding).get("dimension", "")).strip().lower()
   223→        if not dim_key:
   224→            continue
   225→        review_open_by_dim[dim_key] = review_open_by_dim.get(dim_key, 0) + 1
   226→
   227→    items: list[dict] = []
   228→    for entry in subjective_entries:
   229→        name = str(entry.get("name", "")).strip()
   230→        if not name:
   231→            continue
   232→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   233→        if strict_val >= threshold:
   234→            continue
   235→
   236→        [REDACTED](name)
   237→        aliases = set(_subjective_dimension_aliases(name))
   238→        cli_keys = [
   239→            str(key).strip().lower()
   240→            for key in entry.get("cli_keys", [])
   241→            if str(key).strip()
   242→        ]
   243→        aliases.update(cli_keys)
   244→        aliases.update(slugify(key) for key in cli_keys)
   245→        open_review = sum(review_open_by_dim.get(alias, 0) for alias in aliases)
   246→        is_unassessed = bool(entry.get("placeholder")) or (
   247→            name in unassessed_dims
   248→            or (strict_val <= 0.0 and int(entry.get("issues", 0)) == 0)
   249→        )
   250→        is_stale = bool(entry.get("stale"))
   251→        if is_unassessed:
   252→            primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   253→        elif is_stale:
   254→            if cli_keys:
   255→                primary_command = (
   256→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   257→                )
   258→            else:
   259→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   260→        else:
   261→            if open_review > 0:
   262→                primary_command = "desloppify show review --status open"
   263→            elif cli_keys:
   264→                primary_command = (
   265→                    "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions " + ",".join(cli_keys)
   266→                )
   267→            else:
   268→                primary_command = "desloppify review --prepare"
   269→        stale_tag = " [stale — re-review]" if is_stale else ""
   270→        summary = f"Subjective dimension below target: {name} ({strict_val:.1f}%){stale_tag}"
   271→        items.append(
   272→            {
   273→                "id": f"subjective::{slugify(dim_key)}",
   274→                "detector": "subjective_assessment",
   275→                "file": ".",
   276→                "tier": 4,
   277→                "effective_tier": 4,
   278→                "confidence": "medium",
   279→                "summary": summary,
   280→                "detail": {
   281→                    "dimension_name": name,
   282→                    "dimension": dim_key,
   283→                    "issues": int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
   284→                    "strict_score": strict_val,
   285→                    "open_review_findings": open_review,
   286→                    "cli_keys": cli_keys,
   287→                },
   288→                "status": "open",
   289→                "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   290→                "primary_command": primary_command,
   291→            }
   292→        )
   293→    return items
   294→
   295→
   296→def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
   297→    """Build a synthetic T1 queue item for pending epic synthesis.
   298→
   299→    Returns None if synthesis is not needed (no synthesis::pending in queue).
   300→    """
   301→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   302→        SYNTHESIS_ID,
   303→        review_finding_snapshot_hash,
   304→    )
   305→
   306→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   307→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in order:
   308→        return None
   309→
   310→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   311→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   312→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   313→
   314→    # Count review findings
   315→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   316→    review_ids = [
   317→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   318→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   319→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   320→    ]
   321→    total = len(review_ids)
   322→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   323→    new_since_last = sum(1 for fid in review_ids if fid not in synthesized)
   324→    resolved_since_last = sum(1 for fid in synthesized if fid not in {r for r in review_ids})
   325→
   326→    epics = plan.get("epics", {})
   327→    pending_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status", "pending") == "pending")
   328→    in_progress_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") == "in_progress")
   329→    completed_epics = sum(1 for e in epics.values() if e.get("status") == "completed")
   330→
   331→    trigger = "initial synthesis needed"
   332→    if last_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   333→        if new_since_last > resolved_since_last:
   334→            trigger = "new findings imported"
   335→        elif resolved_since_last > 0:
   336→            trigger = "findings resolved"
   337→        else:
   338→            trigger = "findings changed"
   339→
   340→    return {
   341→        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
   342→        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
   343→        "tier": 1,
   344→        "effective_tier": 1,
   345→        "confidence": "high",
   346→        "detector": "synthesis",
   347→        "file": ".",
   348→        "summary": f"Meta-plan update: {total} review findings need integration into epics",
   349→        "status": "open",
   350→        "primary_command": "desloppify plan synthesize",
   351→        "detail": {
   352→            "total_review_findings": total,
   353→            "new_since_last": new_since_last,
   354→            "resolved_since_last": resolved_since_last,
   355→            "trigger": trigger,
   356→            "epic_count": len(epics),
   357→            "pending_epics": pending_epics,
   358→            "in_progress_epics": in_progress_epics,
   359→            "completed_epics": completed_epics,
   360→        },
   361→    }
   362→
   363→
   364→__all__ = [
   365→    "ALL_STATUSES",
   366→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   367→    "build_subjective_items",
   368→    "build_synthesis_item",
   369→    "detail_dict",
   370→    "is_review_finding",
   371→    "is_subjective_finding",
   372→    "primary_command_for_finding",
   373→    "review_finding_weight",
   374→    "scope_matches",
   375→    "slugify",
   376→    "status_matches",
   377→    "subjective_strict_scores",
   378→    "supported_fixers_for_item",
   379→]
   380→
```

```
   327→
   328→        # For review findings, show subjective dimension context
   329→        if item.get("detector") == "review" and dim_scores:
   330→            dim_key = item.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   331→            if dim_key:
   332→                for ds_name, ds_data in dim_scores.items():
   333→                    if ds_name.lower().replace(" ", "_") == dim_key.lower().replace(" ", "_"):
   334→                        score_val = ds_data.get("score", "?")
   335→                        score_str = f"{score_val:.1f}" if isinstance(score_val, (int, float)) else str(score_val)
   336→                        base += f". Subjective dimension: {ds_name} at {score_str}%"
   337→                        break
   338→
   339→        policy = explanation.get("policy")
   340→        if policy:
   341→            base = f"{base}. {policy}"
   342→        print(colorize(f"  explain: {base}", "dim"))
   343→
   344→
   345→def render_queue_header(queue: dict, explain: bool) -> None:
   346→    _render_tier_navigator(queue)
   347→    if not queue.get("fallback_reason"):
   348→        return
   349→    print(colorize(f"  {queue['fallback_reason']}", "yellow"))
   350→    if not explain:
   351→        return
   352→    available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   353→    if available:
   354→        tiers = ", ".join(f"T{tier_num}" for tier_num in available)
   355→        print(colorize(f"  explain: available tiers are {tiers}", "dim"))
   356→
   357→
   358→def show_empty_queue(queue: dict, tier: int | None, strict: float | None) -> bool:
   359→    if queue.get("items"):
   360→        return False
   361→    suffix = f" Strict score: {strict:.1f}/100" if strict is not None else ""
   362→    print(colorize(f"\n  Nothing to do!{suffix}", "green"))
   363→    if tier is not None:
   364→        print(colorize(f"  Requested tier: T{tier}", "dim"))
   365→        available = queue.get("available_tiers", [])
   366→        if available:
   367→            commands = " | ".join(
   368→                f"desloppify next --tier {tier_num}" for tier_num in available
   369→            )
   370→            print(colorize(f"  Try: {commands}", "dim"))
   371→    return True
   372→
   373→
   374→def _render_strategy_summary(plan: dict | None) -> None:
   375→    """Show the meta-plan strategy summary when epics exist."""
   376→    if not plan:
   377→        return
   378→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   379→    summary = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   380→    if not summary:
   381→        return
   382→    print(colorize("\n  Meta-plan:", "bold"))
   383→    for line in summary.strip().splitlines():
   384→        print(colorize(f"  {line}", "dim"))
   385→
   386→
   387→def _render_synthesis_item(item: dict) -> None:
   388→    """Render the synthesis::pending card."""
   389→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   390→    total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
   391→    new = detail.get("new_since_last", 0)
   392→    resolved = detail.get("resolved_since_last", 0)
   393→    trigger = detail.get("trigger", "unknown")
   394→    epic_count = detail.get("epic_count", 0)
   395→    pending = detail.get("pending_epics", 0)
   396→    in_prog = detail.get("in_progress_epics", 0)
   397→    completed = detail.get("completed_epics", 0)
   398→
   399→    print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
   400→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   401→    print(f"  {total} review findings need integration into the meta-plan.")
   402→    print(f"  Trigger: {trigger}")
   403→    print()
   404→    if new:
   405→        print(f"  New findings since last synthesis: {new}")
   406→    if resolved:
   407→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {resolved}")
   408→    if epic_count:
   409→        print(f"  Current epics: {epic_count} ({pending} pending, {in_prog} in progress, {completed} completed)")
   410→    print()
   411→    print(colorize("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow"))
   412→    print()
   413→    print("  Steps:")
   414→    print("    1. Run `desloppify plan synthesize` to see all review findings")
   415→    print("    2. Analyze findings — group by root cause into clusters")
   416→    print("    3. Create clusters, set priorities, focus on first arc")
   417→    print('    4. Mark complete: `desloppify plan done synthesis::pending --note "..." --attest "..."`')
   418→    print("    5. THEN run `desloppify next` for implementation tasks")
   419→    print()
   420→    print("  Preview:  desloppify plan synthesize --dry-run")
   421→    print()
   422→    print("  View plan:")
   423→    print("    desloppify plan queue              — see all items in priority order")
   424→    print("    desloppify plan show               — plan status summary")
   425→
   426→
   427→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   428→    """Render an epic card."""
   429→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   430→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   431→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   432→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   433→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   434→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   435→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   436→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   437→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   438→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   439→
   440→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   441→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   442→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   443→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   444→    if root_cause:
   445→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   446→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   447→    if total_epics:
   448→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   449→    print()
   450→    if action_steps:
   451→        print("  Steps:")
   452→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   453→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   454→        print()
   455→    if finding_ids:
   456→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   457→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   458→        for fid in shown:
   459→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   460→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   461→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   462→        print()
   463→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   464→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   465→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   466→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   467→
   468→
   469→def render_terminal_items(
   470→    items: list[dict],
   471→    dim_scores: dict,
   472→    findings_scoped: dict,
   473→    *,
   474→    group: str,
   475→    explain: bool,
   476→    potentials: dict | None = None,
   477→    plan: dict | None = None,
   478→) -> None:
   479→    # Show strategy summary when epics exist
   480→    _render_strategy_summary(plan)
   481→
   482→    # Show focus header if plan has active cluster
   483→    if plan and plan.get("active_cluster"):
   484→        cluster_name = plan["active_cluster"]
   485→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   486→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   487→        total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   488→        visible = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed")
   489→        print(colorize(f"\n  Focused on: {cluster_name} ({visible} of {total} remaining)", "cyan"))
   490→
   491→        # Exclude the synthesis item from the focus count — it's a queue-level
   492→        # blocker, not a cluster member, so don't count it in "N of M remaining".
   493→        focus_items = [it for it in items if it.get("kind") != "synthesis_needed"]
   494→        if len(focus_items) == 0:
   495→            print(colorize(
   496→                "  This cluster has no remaining items. "
   497→                "Run `desloppify plan focus --clear` to unfocus, "
   498→                "or `desloppify plan cluster show` to inspect.",
   499→                "yellow",
   500→            ))
   501→
   502→    # When synthesis is pending, only show the planning card — don't leak
   503→    # implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip the planning step.
   504→    if items and items[0].get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
   505→        queue_pos = items[0].get("queue_position")
   506→        pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   507→        print(colorize(f"  Next item{pos_str}", "bold"))
   508→        _render_synthesis_item(items[0])
   509→        return
   510→
   511→    if group != "item":
   512→        _render_grouped(items, group)
   513→        return
   514→    for idx, item in enumerate(items):
   515→        if idx > 0:
   516→            print()
   517→        kind = item.get("kind", "")
   518→        queue_pos = item.get("queue_position")
   519→
   520→        # All item types get the same position label — no visual hierarchy
   521→        # that makes synthetic items look like metadata instead of tasks.
   522→        if queue_pos and len(items) > 1:
   523→            label = f"  [#{queue_pos}]"
   524→        elif len(items) > 1:
   525→            label = f"  [{idx + 1}/{len(items)}]"
   526→        else:
   527→            pos_str = f"  (#{ queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
   528→            label = f"  Next item{pos_str}"
   529→        print(colorize(label, "bold"))
   530→
   531→        if kind == "epic":
   532→            _render_epic_item(item)
   533→            continue
   534→        _render_item(item, dim_scores, findings_scoped, explain=explain, potentials=potentials)
   535→
   536→
   537→def render_single_item_resolution_hint(items: list[dict]) -> None:
   538→    if len(items) != 1:
   539→        return
   540→    kind = items[0].get("kind", "")
   541→    if kind in ("cluster", "synthesis_needed", "epic"):
   542→        return  # These cards include their own resolution hints
   543→    if kind != "finding":
   544→        return
   545→    item = items[0]
   546→    detector_name = item.get("detector", "")
   547→    if detector_name == "subjective_review":
   548→        print(colorize("\n  Review with:", "dim"))
   549→        primary = item.get(
   550→            "primary_command", "desloppify show subjective"
   551→        )
   552→        print(f"    {primary}")
   553→        if is_holistic_subjective_finding(item):
   554→            print("    desloppify review --prepare")
   555→        return
   556→
   557→    primary = item.get("primary_command", "")
   558→    if is_auto_fix_command(primary):
   559→        print(colorize("\n  Fix with:", "dim"))
   560→        print(f"    {primary}")
   561→        print(colorize("  Or resolve individually:", "dim"))
   562→    else:
   563→        print(colorize("\n  Resolve with:", "dim"))
   564→
   565→    print(
   566→        f'    desloppify plan done "{item["id"]}" --note "<what you did>" '
   567→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   568→    )
   569→    print(
   570→        f'    desloppify plan skip --permanent "{item["id"]}" --note "<why>" '
   571→        f'--attest "{ATTEST_EXAMPLE}"'
   572→    )
   573→
   574→
   575→def render_followup_nudges(
   576→    state: dict,
   577→    dim_scores: dict,
   578→    findings_scoped: dict,
   579→    *,
   580→    strict_score: float | None,
   581→    target_strict_score: float,
   582→) -> None:
   583→    subjective_threshold = target_strict_score
   584→    subjective_entries = scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
   585→    followup = build_subjective_followup(
   586→        state,
   587→        subjective_entries,
   588→        threshold=subjective_threshold,
   589→        max_quality_items=3,
   590→        max_integrity_items=5,
   591→    )
   592→    unassessed_subjective = unassessed_subjective_dimensions(dim_scores)
   593→    if strict_score is not None:
   594→        gap = round(float(target_strict_score) - float(strict_score), 1)
   595→        if gap > 0:
   596→            print(
   597→                colorize(
   598→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 → target {target_strict_score:.1f} (+{gap:.1f} needed)",
   599→                    "cyan",
   600→                )
   601→            )
   602→        else:
   603→            print(
   604→                colorize(
   605→                    f"\n  North star: strict {strict_score:.1f}/100 meets target {target_strict_score:.1f}",
   606→                    "green",
   607→                )
   608→            )
   609→    # Integrity penalty/warn lines preserved (anti-gaming safeguard, must remain visible).
   610→    for style, message in followup.integrity_lines:
   611→        print(colorize(f"\n  {message}", style))
   612→
   613→    # Collapsed subjective summary.
   614→    coverage_open, _coverage_reasons, _holistic_reasons = subjective_coverage_breakdown(
   615→        findings_scoped
   616→    )
   617→    parts: list[str] = []
   618→    low_dims = len(followup.low_assessed)
   619→    unassessed_count = len(unassessed_subjective)
   620→    stale_count = sum(1 for e in subjective_entries if e.get("stale"))
   621→    open_review = [
   622→        f for f in findings_scoped.values()
   623→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") == "review"
   624→    ]
   625→    if low_dims:
   626→        parts.append(f"{low_dims} dimension{'s' if low_dims != 1 else ''} below target")
   627→    if stale_count:
   628→        parts.append(f"{stale_count} stale")
   629→    if unassessed_count:
   630→        parts.append(f"{unassessed_count} unassessed")
   631→    if len(open_review):
   632→        parts.append(f"{len(open_review)} review finding{'s' if len(open_review) != 1 else ''} open")
   633→    if coverage_open > 0:
   634→        parts.append(f"{coverage_open} file{'s' if coverage_open != 1 else ''} need review")
   635→
   636→    if parts:
   637→        print(colorize(f"\n  Subjective: {', '.join(parts)}.", "cyan"))
   638→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify show subjective` for details.", "dim"))
   639→
   640→
   641→__all__ = [
   642→    "is_auto_fix_command",
   643→    "render_followup_nudges",
   644→    "render_queue_header",
   645→    "render_single_item_resolution_hint",
   646→    "render_terminal_items",
   647→    "scorecard_subjective",
   648→    "show_empty_queue",
   649→    "subjective_coverage_breakdown",
   650→]
   651→
```

```
    38→
    39→
    40→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    41→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    42→    try:
    43→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    44→    except OSError as exc:
    45→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    46→        sys.exit(1)
    47→
    48→
    49→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    50→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    51→    try:
    52→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    53→    except OSError as exc:
    54→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    55→        sys.exit(1)
    56→
    57→
    58→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    59→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    60→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    61→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    62→
    63→    state_file = state_path(args)
    64→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    65→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    66→        state,
    67→        args,
    68→        attestation=attestation,
    69→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    70→    )
    71→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    72→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    73→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    74→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    75→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    79→    if not all_resolved:
    80→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    81→        print(
    82→            colorize(
    83→                f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}",
    84→                "yellow",
    85→            )
    86→        )
    87→        return
    88→
    89→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    90→
    91→    # Remove resolved items from the living plan queue + update epic progress
    92→    try:
    93→        from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan, purge_ids, save_plan
    94→
    95→        if has_living_plan():
    96→            plan = load_plan()
    97→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    98→            # Track epic progress
    99→            epic_changed = False
   100→            for epic_name, epic in plan.get("epics", {}).items():
   101→                epic_fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   102→                hits = [fid for fid in all_resolved if fid in epic_fids]
   103→                if hits:
   104→                    for fid in hits:
   105→                        epic_fids.remove(fid)
   106→                    epic_changed = True
   107→                    remaining = len(epic_fids)
   108→                    if remaining == 0:
   109→                        print(colorize(f"  Epic {epic_name}: completed!", "green"))
   110→                        epic["status"] = "completed"
   111→                    else:
   112→                        print(colorize(
   113→                            f"  Epic {epic_name}: {remaining} finding(s) remaining.",
   114→                            "dim",
   115→                        ))
   116→            # Nudge about synthesis staleness for review findings
   117→            has_review = any(
   118→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   119→                for fid in all_resolved
   120→            )
   121→            if has_review:
   122→                from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
   123→                if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   124→                    print(colorize(
   125→                        "  Tip: review findings changed -- run "
   126→                        "`desloppify plan synthesize` to update the meta-plan.",
   127→                        "yellow",
   128→                    ))
   129→            if purged or epic_changed:
   130→                save_plan(plan)
   131→            if purged:
   132→                print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
   133→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   134→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   135→
   136→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   137→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   138→        state,
   139→        status=args.status,
   140→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   141→    )
   142→    has_review_findings = any(
   143→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   144→        for fid in all_resolved
   145→    )
   146→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
   147→
   148→    _resolve_config = config_mod.load_config()
   149→    _resolve_target = target_strict_score_from_config(_resolve_config, fallback=95.0)
   150→    _print_score_movement(
   151→        status=args.status,
   152→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   153→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   154→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   155→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   156→        state=state,
   157→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
   158→        target_strict=_resolve_target,
   159→    )
   160→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(

   164→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   165→    )
   166→
   167→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   168→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   169→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   170→        state,
   171→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   172→    )
   173→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   174→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   175→
   176→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   177→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   178→        ResolveQueryContext(
   179→            patterns=args.patterns,
   180→            status=args.status,

   182→            next_command=next_command,

   186→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   187→            attestation=attestation,
   188→            narrative=narrative,
   189→            state=state,
   190→        )
   191→    )

   194→def cmd_ignore_pattern(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   195→    """Add a pattern to the ignore list."""
   196→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   197→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   198→        show_attestation_requirement("Ignore", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   199→        sys.exit(1)
   200→
   201→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   202→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   203→    state = runtime.state
   204→    prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   205→
   206→    config = runtime.config
   207→    config_mod.add_ignore_pattern(config, args.pattern)
   208→    config["needs_rescan"] = True
   209→    _save_config_or_exit(config)
   210→
   211→    removed = state_mod.remove_ignored_findings(state, args.pattern)
   212→    state.setdefault("attestation_log", []).append(
   213→        {
   214→            "timestamp": state.get("last_scan"),
   215→            "command": "ignore",
   216→            "pattern": args.pattern,
   217→            "attestation": attestation,
   218→            "affected": removed,
   219→        }
   220→    )
   221→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
   222→
   223→    print(colorize(f"Added ignore pattern: {args.pattern}", "green"))
   224→    if removed:
   225→        print(f"  Removed {removed} matching findings from state.")
   226→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   227→    if config_warning:
   228→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   229→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   230→
   231→    lang = resolve_lang(args)

   233→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(

   246→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
```

```
    34→def _show_concerns(state: dict, lang_name: str | None) -> None:
    35→    """Render current design concerns from mechanical signals."""
    36→    from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
    37→
    38→    concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
    39→    if not concerns:
    40→        print(colorize("  No design concerns detected.", "green"))
    41→        return
    42→
    43→    print(colorize(f"\n  {len(concerns)} design concern(s):\n", "bold"))
    44→    dismissals = state.get("concern_dismissals", {})
    45→
    46→    for i, c in enumerate(concerns, 1):
    47→        print(colorize(f"  {i}. [{c.type}] {c.file}", "cyan"))
    48→        print(f"     {c.summary}")
    49→        for ev in c.evidence:
    50→            print(colorize(f"       - {ev}", "dim"))
    51→        print(colorize(f"     ? {c.question}", "yellow"))
    52→
    53→        # Check if previously dismissed (but resurface due to changed findings).
    54→        prev = dismissals.get(c.fingerprint)
    55→        if isinstance(prev, dict):
    56→            reasoning = prev.get("reasoning", "")
    57→            if reasoning:
    58→                print(colorize(f"     (previously dismissed: {reasoning})", "dim"))
    59→        print()

    62→def _print_dimension_score(dim_data: dict, display_name: str) -> None:

    72→                "bold",

   100→        and lowered

   117→def _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state: dict, entity) -> None:
   118→    """Show score + 'no open findings' for a mechanical dimension with zero findings."""
   119→    dim_data, display_name = _lookup_dimension_score(state, entity.display_name)
   120→    _print_dimension_score(dim_data, display_name)
   121→    det_list = ", ".join(entity.detectors) if entity.detectors else "none"
   122→    print(

   130→def _load_dimension_findings(
   131→    state: dict,
   132→    entity,
   133→    status_filter: str,
   134→) -> list[dict]:
   135→    """Load findings for all detectors in a mechanical dimension."""
   136→    all_matches: list[dict] = []
   137→    for detector in entity.detectors:
   138→        matches = load_matches(
   139→            state, scope=detector, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=False
   140→        )
   141→        all_matches.extend(matches)
   142→    # Deduplicate by finding ID
   143→    seen: set[str] = set()
   144→    unique: list[dict] = []
   145→    for item in all_matches:
   146→        fid = item.get("id", "")
   147→        if fid not in seen:
   148→            seen.add(fid)
   149→            unique.append(item)
   150→    return unique
   151→
```
