```
   191→        print(colorize("    `show review --status open`            Per-file design review findings", "dim"))
   192→        print(colorize("    `show subjective_review --status open`  Files needing re-review", "dim"))
   193→
   194→
   195→def cmd_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   196→    """Show all findings for a file, directory, detector, or pattern."""
   197→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   198→    state = runtime.state
   199→    config = runtime.config
   200→
   201→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   202→        return
   203→
   204→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
   205→    if stale_warning:
   206→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
   207→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
   208→    if skill_warning:
   209→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
   210→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
   211→    if config_warning:
   212→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
   213→
   214→    pattern_raw = getattr(args, "pattern", "")
   215→    show_code = getattr(args, "code", False)
   216→    chronic = getattr(args, "chronic", False)
   217→    ok, pattern, status_filter, scope = resolve_show_scope(args)
   218→    if not ok or pattern is None:
   219→        return
   220→
   221→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   222→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   223→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   224→        state,
   225→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="show"),
   226→    )
   227→
   228→    # --- Entity resolution: classify what the user asked for ---
   229→    entity = resolve_entity(pattern, state)
   230→
   231→    # Dispatch: concerns special view
   232→    if entity.kind == "special_view" and entity.pattern.strip().lower() == "concerns":
   233→        _show_concerns(state, lang_name)
   234→        return
   235→
   236→    # Dispatch: subjective dimension
   237→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and entity.is_subjective:
   238→        _render_subjective_dimension(state, config, entity, pattern_raw)
   239→        return
   240→
   241→    # Dispatch: mechanical dimension
   242→    if entity.kind == "dimension" and not entity.is_subjective:
   243→        matches = _load_dimension_findings(state, entity, status_filter)
   244→        if not matches:
   245→            _render_clean_mechanical_dimension(state, entity)
   246→            return
   247→        pattern = entity.display_name
   248→    else:
   249→        # All other kinds: existing load_matches path
   250→        matches = load_matches(
   251→            state, scope=scope, status_filter=status_filter, chronic=chronic
   252→        )
   253→
   254→    if not matches:
   255→        _render_no_matches(entity, pattern, status_filter, narrative, state, config)
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    (
   259→        surfaced_matches,
   260→        hidden_by_detector,
   261→        noise_budget,
   262→        global_noise_budget,
   263→        budget_warning,
   264→    ) = resolve_noise(
   265→        config,
   266→        matches,
   267→    )
   268→    hidden_total = sum(hidden_by_detector.values())
   269→
   270→    payload = build_show_payload(
   271→        surfaced_matches,
   272→        pattern,
   273→        status_filter,
   274→        ShowPayloadMeta(
   275→            total_matches=len(matches),
   276→            hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   277→            noise_budget=noise_budget,
   278→            global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   279→        ),
   280→    )
   281→    write_query({"command": "show", **payload, "narrative": narrative})
   282→
   283→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   284→    if output_file:
   285→        if write_show_output_file(output_file, payload, len(surfaced_matches)):
   286→            return
   287→        raise SystemExit(1)
   288→
   289→    top = getattr(args, "top", 20) or 20
   290→    render_findings(
   291→        surfaced_matches,
   292→        pattern=pattern,
   293→        status_filter=status_filter,
   294→        show_code=show_code,
   295→        top=top,
   296→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   297→        hidden_total=hidden_total,
   298→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   299→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   300→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   301→    )
   302→    try:
   303→        from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan as _load_plan
   304→        _plan = _load_plan()
   305→        _plan_active = _plan if (_plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters")) else None
   306→    except Exception:
   307→        _plan_active = None
   308→    show_agent_plan(narrative, surfaced_matches, plan=_plan_active)
   309→    show_subjective_followup(
   310→        state,
   311→        target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0),
   312→    )
   313→
   314→    _render_subjective_views_guide(entity)
   315→
```

```
1→"""fix command: auto-fix mechanical issues with fixer registry and pipeline."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from pathlib import Path
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands._show_terminal import show_fix_dry_run_samples
     9→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import FixResult
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    11→
    12→from .apply_flow import (
    13→    _apply_and_report,
    14→    _detect,
    15→    _print_fix_summary,
    16→    _report_dry_run,
    17→    _warn_uncommitted_changes,
    18→)
    19→from .options import _load_fixer
    20→from .review_flow import _cmd_fix_review
    21→
    22→
    23→def cmd_fix(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    24→    """Auto-fix mechanical issues."""
    25→    fixer_name = args.fixer
    26→    if fixer_name == "review":
    27→        _cmd_fix_review(args)
    28→        return
    29→
    30→    dry_run = getattr(args, "dry_run", False)
    31→    path = Path(args.path)
    32→
    33→    lang, fixer = _load_fixer(args, fixer_name)
    34→
    35→    if not dry_run:
    36→        _warn_uncommitted_changes()
    37→    entries = _detect(fixer, path)
    38→    if not entries:
    39→        print(colorize(f"No {fixer.label} found.", "green"))
    40→        return
    41→
    42→    raw = fixer.fix(entries, dry_run=dry_run)
    43→    if isinstance(raw, FixResult):
    44→        results = raw.entries
    45→        skip_reasons = raw.skip_reasons
    46→    else:
    47→        results = raw
    48→        skip_reasons = {}
    49→    total_items = sum(len(r["removed"]) for r in results)
    50→    total_lines = sum(r.get("lines_removed", 0) for r in results)
    51→    _print_fix_summary(fixer, results, total_items, total_lines, dry_run)
    52→
    53→    if dry_run and results:
    54→        show_fix_dry_run_samples(entries, results)
    55→
    56→    if not dry_run:
    57→        _apply_and_report(
    58→            args,
    59→            path,
    60→            fixer,
    61→            fixer_name,
    62→            entries,
    63→            results,
    64→            total_items,
    65→            lang,
    66→            skip_reasons,
    67→        )
    68→    else:
    69→        _report_dry_run(args, fixer_name, entries, results, total_items)
    70→    print()
    71→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
```

```
100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   105→    plan: PlanModel,
   106→    state: StateModel,
   107→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   108→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   109→
   110→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   111→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   112→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   113→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   114→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   115→    """
   116→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   117→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   118→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   119→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   120→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   121→
   122→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   123→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   124→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   125→        fid for fid in order
   126→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   127→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   128→        and fid not in stale_ids
   129→    ]
   130→    for fid in to_remove:
   131→        order.remove(fid)
   132→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   133→
   134→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front ----------------------------
   135→    existing = set(order)
   136→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   137→        if uid not in existing:
   138→            order.insert(0, uid)
   139→            result.injected.append(uid)
   140→
   141→    return result
   142→
   143→
   144→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   145→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   146→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   147→
   148→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   149→    plan: PlanModel,
   150→    state: StateModel,
   151→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   152→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   153→
   154→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   155→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   156→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   157→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   158→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   159→    """
   160→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   161→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   162→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   163→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   164→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   165→
   166→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   167→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   168→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   169→        fid for fid in order
   170→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   171→        and fid not in stale_ids
   172→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   173→    ]
   174→    for fid in to_remove:
   175→        order.remove(fid)
   176→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   177→
   178→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   179→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   180→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   181→        existing = set(order)
   182→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   183→            if sid not in existing:
   184→                order.append(sid)
   185→                result.injected.append(sid)
   186→
   187→    return result
   188→
   189→
   190→@dataclass
   191→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   192→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   193→
   194→    injected: bool = False
   195→    pruned: bool = False
   196→
   197→    @property
   198→    def changes(self) -> int:
   199→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   200→
   201→
   202→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   203→    """Hash of sorted open review/concerns finding IDs."""
   204→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   205→    ids = sorted(
   206→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   207→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   208→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   209→    )
   210→    if not ids:
   211→        return ""
   212→    return hashlib.md5("|".join(ids).encode()).hexdigest()
   213→
   214→
   215→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   216→    plan: PlanModel,
   217→    state: StateModel,
   218→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   219→    """Inject/prune synthesis::pending based on review finding drift.
   220→
   221→    Triggers when open review/concerns findings exist AND the snapshot
   222→    hash differs from the last-synthesized hash.
   223→    """
   224→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   225→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   226→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   227→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   228→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   229→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   230→    has_synthesis = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   231→
   232→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
   233→        # Need synthesis — inject if not present
   234→        if not has_synthesis:
   235→            order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   236→            result.injected = True
   237→    else:
   238→        # Synthesis up to date or no review findings — prune if present
   239→        if has_synthesis:
   240→            order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   241→            result.pruned = True
   242→
   243→    return result
   244→
   245→
   246→__all__ = [
   247→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   248→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   249→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   250→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   251→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   252→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   253→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   254→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   255→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   256→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   257→]
   258→
```

```
1→"""Rendering and output helpers for show command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.rendering import print_ranked_actions
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.subjective import print_subjective_followup
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.scan import (
    11→    scan_reporting_dimensions as reporting_dimensions_mod,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→from desloppify.core.paths_api import read_code_snippet
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    16→from desloppify.core.enums import canonical_finding_status
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    19→
    20→from .formatting import format_detail
    21→
    22→
    23→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    24→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    25→    try:
    26→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
    27→        print(colorize(f"Wrote {surfaced_count} findings to {output_file}", "green"))
    28→    except OSError as exc:
    29→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
    30→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="show output")
    31→        return False
    32→    return True
    33→
    34→
    35→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    36→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    37→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    38→    for finding in matches:
    39→        by_file[finding["file"]].append(finding)
    40→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))
    41→
    42→
    43→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    44→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    45→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    46→    status_icon = {
    47→        "open": "○",
    48→        "fixed": "✓",
    49→        "wontfix": "—",
    50→        "false_positive": "✗",
    51→        "auto_resolved": "◌",
    52→    }.get(normalized_status, "?")
    53→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")
    54→    zone_tag = colorize(f" [{zone}]", "dim") if zone != "production" else ""
    55→    print(
    56→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"
    57→    )
    58→
    59→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))
    60→    if detail_parts:
    61→        print(colorize(f"      {' · '.join(detail_parts)}", "dim"))
    62→    if show_code:
    63→        detail = finding.get("detail", {})
    64→        target_line = (
    65→            detail.get("line") or (detail.get("lines", [None]) or [None])[0]
    66→        )
    67→        if target_line and finding["file"] not in (".", ""):
    68→            snippet = read_code_snippet(finding["file"], target_line)
    69→            if snippet:
    70→                print(snippet)
    71→    if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2:
    72→        print(
    73→            colorize(
    74→                f"      ⟳ reopened {finding['reopen_count']} times — fix properly or wontfix",
    75→                "red",
    76→            )
    77→        )
    78→    if finding.get("note"):
    79→        print(colorize(f"      note: {finding['note']}", "dim"))
    80→    print(colorize(f"      {finding['id']}", "dim"))
    81→
    82→
    83→def render_findings(
    84→    matches: list[dict],
    85→    *,
    86→    pattern: str,
    87→    status_filter: str,
    88→    show_code: bool,
    89→    top: int,
    90→    hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    91→    hidden_total: int,
    92→    noise_budget: int,
    93→    global_noise_budget: int,
    94→    budget_warning: str | None,
    95→) -> None:
    96→    """Render grouped findings and rollup summary to terminal."""
    97→    sorted_files = group_matches_by_file(matches)
    98→    print(
    99→        colorize(
   100→            f"\n  {len(matches)} {status_filter} findings matching '{pattern}'\n",
   101→            "bold",
   102→        )
   103→    )
   104→    if budget_warning:
   105→        print(colorize(f"  {budget_warning}\n", "yellow"))
   106→    if hidden_total:
   107→        global_label = (
   108→            f", {global_noise_budget} global" if global_noise_budget > 0 else ""
   109→        )
   110→        hidden_parts = ", ".join(
   111→            f"{det}: +{count}" for det, count in hidden_by_detector.items()
   112→        )
   113→        print(
   114→            colorize(
   115→                (
   116→                    "  Noise budget: "

   118→                    f"({hidden_total} hidden: {hidden_parts})\n"
   119→                ),
   120→                "dim",
   121→            )
   122→        )
   123→
   124→    shown_files = sorted_files[:top]
   125→    remaining_files = sorted_files[top:]
   126→    remaining_findings = sum(len(files) for _, files in remaining_files)
   127→
   128→    for filepath, findings in shown_files:
   129→        findings.sort(
   130→            key=lambda finding: (
   131→                finding["tier"],
   132→                CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(finding["confidence"], 9),
   133→            )

   139→        )
   140→
   141→        for finding in findings:

   145→    if remaining_findings:

   152→                ),
   153→                "dim",
   154→            )
   155→        )

   158→    by_tier: dict[int, int] = defaultdict(int)
   159→    for finding in matches:
   160→        by_detector[finding["detector"]] += 1
   161→        by_tier[finding["tier"]] += 1
   162→
   163→    print(colorize("  Summary:", "bold"))
   164→    print(
   165→        colorize(
   166→            (
   167→                "    By tier:     "
   168→                + ", ".join(
   169→                    f"T{tier}:{count}" for tier, count in sorted(by_tier.items())
   170→                )
   171→            ),
   172→            "dim",
   173→        )
   174→    )
   175→    print(

   181→                    for detector, count in sorted(

   183→                    )
   184→                )
   185→            ),

   189→    if hidden_total:

   253→def show_subjective_followup(state: dict, target_strict_score: float) -> None:
   254→    """Show subjective follow-up guidance for the current state."""
   255→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   256→    if not dim_scores:
   257→        return
   258→
   259→    subjective = reporting_dimensions_mod.scorecard_subjective_entries(
   260→        state,
   261→        dim_scores=dim_scores,
   262→    )
   263→    if not subjective:
   264→        return
   265→
   266→    followup = reporting_dimensions_mod.build_subjective_followup(
   267→        state,
   268→        subjective,
   269→        threshold=target_strict_score,
   270→        max_quality_items=3,
   271→        max_integrity_items=5,
   272→    )
   273→    if print_subjective_followup(followup):
   274→        print()
```

```
    17→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import CONFIDENCE_ORDER
    18→from desloppify.state import path_scoped_findings
    19→
    20→
    21→def subjective_score_value(item: dict) -> float:
    22→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
    23→        detail = detail_dict(item)
    24→        return float(detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0)))
    25→    return float(item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    26→
    27→
    28→def build_finding_items(
    29→    state: dict,
    30→    *,
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→    status_filter: str,
    33→    scope: str | None,
    34→    chronic: bool,
    35→) -> list[dict]:
    36→    scoped = path_scoped_findings(state.get("findings", {}), scan_path)
    37→    subjective_scores = subjective_strict_scores(state)
    38→    out: list[dict] = []
    39→
    40→    for finding_id, finding in scoped.items():
    41→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    42→            continue
    43→        if not status_matches(finding.get("status", "open"), status_filter):
    44→            continue
    45→        if chronic and not (
    46→            finding.get("status") == "open" and finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2
    47→        ):
    48→            continue
    49→
    50→        item = dict(finding)
    51→        item["id"] = finding_id
    52→        item["kind"] = "finding"
    53→        item["is_review"] = is_review_finding(item)
    54→        item["is_subjective"] = is_subjective_finding(item)
    55→        item["effective_tier"] = (
    56→            4 if item["is_subjective"] else int(finding.get("tier", 3))
    57→        )
    58→        item["review_weight"] = (
    59→            review_finding_weight(item) if item["is_review"] else None
    60→        )
    61→        subjective_score = None
    62→        if item["is_subjective"]:
    63→            detail = detail_dict(finding)
    64→            dim_name = detail.get("dimension_name", "")
    65→            dim_key = detail.get("dimension", "") or slugify(dim_name)
    66→            subjective_score = subjective_scores.get(
    67→                dim_key, subjective_scores.get(dim_name.lower(), 100.0)
    68→            )
    69→        item["subjective_score"] = subjective_score
    70→        supported_fixers = supported_fixers_for_item(state, item)
    71→        item["primary_command"] = primary_command_for_finding(
    72→            item,
    73→            supported_fixers=supported_fixers,
    74→        )
    75→
    76→        if not scope_matches(item, scope):
    77→            continue
    78→        out.append(item)
    79→
    80→    return out
    81→
    82→
    83→_CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
    84→
    85→
    86→def item_sort_key(item: dict) -> tuple:
    87→    kind = item.get("kind", "")
    88→
    89→    # Synthesis and epic items sort before everything else (T0-equivalent).
    90→    if kind == "synthesis_needed":
    91→        return (-2, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    92→    if kind == "epic":
    93→        dep_order = 999
    94→        detail = item.get("detail")
    95→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    96→            dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
    97→        return (-1, dep_order, 0, item.get("id", ""))
    98→
    99→    if kind == "cluster":
   100→        # Clusters sort before individual findings, ordered by action type
   101→        # (auto_fix first, then reorganize, refactor, manual_fix),
   102→        # then bigger clusters first within the same action type.
   103→        action_pri = _CLUSTER_ACTION_PRIORITY.get(
   104→            item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"), 3
   105→        )
   106→        return (
   107→            0,  # All clusters before all individual findings
   108→            action_pri,
   109→            -int(item.get("member_count", 0)),
   110→            item.get("id", ""),
   111→        )
   112→
   113→    if kind == "subjective_dimension" or item.get("is_subjective"):
   114→        return (
   115→            int(item.get("effective_tier", 4)),
   116→            1,  # Subjective items sort after mechanical items within T4.
   117→            subjective_score_value(item),
   118→            item.get("id", ""),
   119→        )
   120→
   121→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   122→    review_weight = float(item.get("review_weight", 0.0) or 0.0)
   123→    return (
   124→        int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3))),
   125→        0,
   126→        CONFIDENCE_ORDER.get(item.get("confidence", "low"), 9),
   127→        -review_weight,
   128→        -int(detail.get("count", 0) or 0),
   129→        item.get("id", ""),
   130→    )
   131→
   132→
   133→def item_explain(item: dict) -> dict:
   134→    effective_tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   135→
   136→    if item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension":
   137→        return {
   138→            "kind": "subjective_dimension",
   139→            "effective_tier": effective_tier,
   140→            "subjective_score": subjective_score_value(item),
   141→            "policy": (
   142→                "Subjective dimensions are always queued as T4 and do not outrank "
   143→                "mechanical T1/T2/T3 items."
   144→            ),
   145→            "ranking_factors": ["tier asc", "subjective_score asc", "id asc"],
   146→        }
   147→
   148→    detail = detail_dict(item)
   149→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "low")
   150→    is_subjective = bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
```

```
1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    13→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    14→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    15→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    16→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    17→        serialized_members = [serialize_item(m) for m in members_raw]
    18→        return {
    19→            "id": item.get("id"),
    20→            "kind": "cluster",
    21→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    22→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    23→            "member_count": item.get("member_count", len(serialized_members)),
    24→            "members": serialized_members,
    25→            "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    26→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    27→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    28→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    29→        }
    30→
    31→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    32→        "id": item.get("id"),
    33→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    34→        "tier": item.get("tier"),
    35→        "effective_tier": item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier")),
    36→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    37→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    38→        "file": item.get("file"),
    39→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    40→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    41→        "status": item.get("status"),
    42→        "primary_command": item.get("primary_command"),
    43→    }
    44→    explain = item.get("explain")
    45→    if explain is not None:
    46→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    47→
    48→    # Plan metadata
    49→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    50→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    51→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    52→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    53→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    54→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    55→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    56→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    57→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    58→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    59→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    60→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    61→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    62→
    63→    return serialized
    64→
    65→
    66→def build_query_payload(
    67→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    68→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    69→    *,
    70→    command: str,
    71→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
    72→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    73→) -> dict[str, Any]:
    74→    """Build JSON payload for query.json and non-terminal output modes."""
    75→    serialized = [serialize_item(item) for item in items]
    76→    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
    77→        "command": command,
    78→        "items": serialized,
    79→        "queue": {
    80→            "tier_counts": queue.get("tier_counts", {}),
    81→            "requested_tier": queue.get("requested_tier"),
    82→            "selected_tier": queue.get("selected_tier"),
    83→            "fallback_reason": queue.get("fallback_reason"),
    84→            "available_tiers": queue.get("available_tiers", []),
    85→            "total": queue.get("total", len(items)),
    86→        },
    87→        "narrative": narrative,
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    if plan and (
    91→        plan.get("queue_order")
    92→        or plan.get("skipped")
    93→        or plan.get("clusters")
    94→    ):
    95→        clusters_summary = []
    96→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    97→            member_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    98→            clusters_summary.append({
    99→                "name": name,
   100→                "description": cluster.get("description"),
   101→                "item_count": len(member_ids),
   102→            })
   103→        payload["plan"] = {
   104→            "active": True,
   105→            "focus": plan.get("active_cluster"),
   106→            "clusters": clusters_summary,
   107→            "total_ordered": len(plan.get("queue_order", [])),
   108→            "total_skipped": len(plan.get("skipped", {})),
   109→            "plan_overrides_narrative": True,
   110→        }
   111→
   112→    return payload
   113→
   114→
   115→def render_markdown(items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> str:
   116→    """Render queue items as markdown."""
   117→    lines = [
   118→        "# Desloppify Next Queue",
   119→        "",
   120→        "| Kind | Tier | Confidence | Summary | Command |",
   121→        "|------|------|------------|---------|---------|",
   122→    ]
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
   125→        tier = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   126→        conf = item.get("confidence", "medium")
   127→        summary = item.get("summary", "").replace("|", "\\|")
   128→        command = (item.get("primary_command", "") or "").replace("|", "\\|")
   129→        lines.append(f"| {kind} | T{tier} | {conf} | {summary} | {command} |")
   130→    lines.append("")
   131→    return "\n".join(lines)
   132→
   133→
   134→def write_output_file(
   135→    output_file: str,
   136→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   137→    item_count: int,
   138→    *,
   139→    safe_write_text_fn,
   140→    colorize_fn,
   141→) -> bool:
   142→    """Persist payload to file and print success/failure hints."""
   143→    try:
   144→        safe_write_text_fn(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")
   145→        print(colorize_fn(f"Wrote {item_count} items to {output_file}", "green"))
   146→    except OSError as exc:
   147→        payload["output_error"] = str(exc)
   148→        print_write_error(output_file, exc, label="next output")
   149→        return False
   150→    return True
   151→
   152→
   153→def emit_non_terminal_output(
   154→    output_format: str,
   155→    payload: dict[str, Any],
   156→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
   157→) -> bool:
   158→    """Render JSON/markdown output variants."""
   159→    renderers = {
   160→        "json": lambda: print(json.dumps(payload, indent=2)),
   161→        "md": lambda: print(render_markdown(items)),
   162→    }
   163→    renderer = renderers.get(output_format)
   164→    if renderer is None:
   165→        return False
   166→    renderer()
   167→    return True
   168→
   169→
   170→__all__ = [
   171→    "build_query_payload",
   172→    "emit_non_terminal_output",
   173→    "render_markdown",
   174→    "serialize_item",
   175→    "write_output_file",
   176→]
   177→
```

```
    29→def _scorecard_subjective(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    dim_scores: dict,
    32→) -> list[dict]:
    33→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    34→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    35→
    36→
    37→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    38→    state: dict,
    39→    dim_scores: dict,
    40→    *,
    41→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    42→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    43→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    44→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    45→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    46→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    47→            continue
    48→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    49→        if strict_val < threshold:
    50→            low.append(
    51→                (
    52→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    53→                    strict_val,
    54→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    55→                )
    56→            )
    57→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    58→    return low
    59→
    60→
    61→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    62→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    63→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    64→    state = runtime.state
    65→    config = runtime.config
    66→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    67→        return
    68→
    69→    stale_warning = check_tool_staleness(state)
    70→    if stale_warning:
    71→        print(colorize(f"  {stale_warning}", "yellow"))
    72→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    73→    if skill_warning:
    74→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    75→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    76→    if config_warning:
    77→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    78→
    79→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    80→
    81→
    82→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
    83→    tier = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    84→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
    85→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
    86→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
    87→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
    88→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
    89→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
    90→    no_tier_fallback = bool(getattr(args, "no_tier_fallback", False))
    91→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    92→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
    93→
    94→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    95→
    96→    # Load the living plan
    97→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    98→    plan = load_plan()
    99→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   100→    if (
   101→        plan.get("queue_order")
   102→        or plan.get("overrides")
   103→        or plan.get("clusters")
   104→    ):
   105→        plan_data = plan
   106→
   107→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   108→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
   109→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
   110→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
   111→        if active_cluster:
   112→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   113→
   114→    queue = build_work_queue(
   115→        state,
   116→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   117→            tier=tier,
   118→            count=count,
   119→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   120→            scope=scope,
   121→            status=status,
   122→            include_subjective=True,
   123→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   124→            no_tier_fallback=no_tier_fallback,
   125→            explain=explain,
   126→            plan=plan_data,
   127→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   128→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   129→        ),
   130→    )
   131→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   132→
   133→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   134→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   135→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   136→        state,
   137→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   138→    )
   139→
   140→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   141→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   142→    )
   143→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   144→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   145→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   146→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   147→        state,
   148→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   149→    )
   150→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [

   152→    ]
   153→    write_query(payload)
   154→

   158→            output_file,
   159→            payload,
   160→            len(items),

   162→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   163→        ):
   164→            return

   168→        return
   169→
   170→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)

   179→    )
```

```
427→def _render_epic_item(item: dict) -> None:
   428→    """Render an epic card."""
   429→    detail = item.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(item.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   430→    direction = detail.get("direction", "")
   431→    thesis = detail.get("thesis", "")
   432→    root_cause = detail.get("root_cause", "")
   433→    status = detail.get("epic_status", "pending")
   434→    agent_safe = detail.get("agent_safe", False)
   435→    dep_order = detail.get("dependency_order", 999)
   436→    total_epics = detail.get("total_epics", 0)
   437→    finding_ids = detail.get("finding_ids", [])
   438→    action_steps = detail.get("action_steps", [])
   439→
   440→    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
   441→    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
   442→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
   443→    print(f"  Thesis: {thesis}")
   444→    if root_cause:
   445→        print(f"  Root cause: {root_cause}")
   446→    print(f"  Status: {status}")
   447→    if total_epics:
   448→        print(f"  This is part of the meta-plan (epic {dep_order} of {total_epics}).")
   449→    print()
   450→    if action_steps:
   451→        print("  Steps:")
   452→        for i, step in enumerate(action_steps, 1):
   453→            print(f"    {i}. {step}")
   454→        print()
   455→    if finding_ids:
   456→        shown = finding_ids[:5]
   457→        print("  Findings in this epic:")
   458→        for fid in shown:
   459→            print(f"    - {fid}")
   460→        if len(finding_ids) > 5:
   461→            print(f"    ... and {len(finding_ids) - 5} more")
   462→        print()
   463→    epic_name = item.get("id", "")
   464→    print(f"  Work on this epic:")
   465→    print(f"    desloppify plan focus {epic_name}")
   466→    print(f"    (then work through findings with desloppify next)")
   467→
   468→
```

## Comprehensive Work Queue Pipeline Analysis

Based on my exploration of the codebase, here's a detailed breakdown of how synthesis_needed items flow through the system and the natural gating points:

---

### 1. **SYNTHESIS_ID & Core Identity**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:20`

```python
SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
```

This constant represents the ONE special T1 queue item that blocks all other implementation work when present.

---

### 2. **Work Queue Build & Item Filtering** 

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

#### **Key Finding: `count` parameter respects synthesis items as #1**

Lines 255-276 show `_inject_synthesis_and_epic_items()`:
- Checks if `SYNTHESIS_ID` is in `plan.get("queue_order", [])`
- If yes, builds a synthesis_item object via `build_synthesis_item(plan, state)` (lines 265-269)
- Appends it to `all_items` list
- This happens **before** sorting and before `count` truncation

Lines 427-482 show the full build pipeline:
1. **Line 427:** Items are collected and sorted via `item_sort_key`
2. **Lines 420-425:** Synthesis + epic items are injected into `all_items`
3. **Lines 429-437:** Plan ordering is applied
4. **Lines 440-447:** Auto-clusters are collapsed
5. **Line 481:** `count` parameter truncates: `filtered = filtered[: resolved_options.count]`

**Critical behavior:** If `count=5` and synthesis_needed is #1, you get:
- Item #1: synthesis_needed
- Items #2-5: next 4 items from the queue

The synthesis item **always** comes first due to its sort key (line 91 in ranking.py):

```python
if kind == "synthesis_needed":
    return (-2, 0, 0, item.get("id", ""))  # Sorts BEFORE epics (-1) and all other items
```

---

### 3. **Ranking & Sort Order**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/ranking.py:86-130`

Synthesis items get sort key `(-2, 0, 0, id)`, placing them BEFORE:
- Epics: `(-1, dependency_order, 0, id)`
- Clusters: `(0, action_priority, -member_count, id)`
- Regular findings: `(tier, 0/1, confidence, ...)`

---

### 4. **Rendering & Agent Behavior Gating** 

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py:469-535`

#### **Critical Gate: `render_terminal_items()` lines 504-509**

```python
# When synthesis is pending, only show the planning card — don't leak
# implementation tasks that would tempt agents to skip the planning step.
if items and items[0].get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
    queue_pos = items[0].get("queue_position")
    pos_str = f"  (#{queue_pos} in queue)" if queue_pos else ""
    print(colorize(f"  Next item{pos_str}", "bold"))
    _render_synthesis_item(items[0])
    return
```

**This is the PRIMARY GATE:** When synthesis_needed is present:
1. Only the synthesis card is rendered (lines 387-425)
2. **No regular items are shown** — the function returns early at line 509
3. This prevents agents from seeing implementation tasks and being tempted to skip planning

The synthesis card explicitly instructs (lines 399-425):
```
PLAN — do not proceed until complete
This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.
```

---

### 5. **Cluster Filtering (Synthesis Immunity)**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py:156-166`

When a cluster is active, synthesis items are **excluded from cluster filtering** — they always appear:

```python
# Filter to cluster if requested — but never filter out synthesis items;
# they are queue-level blockers that outrank cluster membership.
effective_cluster = cluster or active_cluster
if effective_cluster:
    cluster_data = clusters.get(effective_cluster, {})
    cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    result = [
        item for item in result
        if item["id"] in cluster_member_ids
        or item.get("kind") == "synthesis_needed"  # <-- ALWAYS KEPT
    ]
```

---

### 6. **How Synthesis Becomes Pending**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py:215-243`

The `sync_synthesis_needed()` function is called **after every scan** (scan_workflow.py:483):

```python
def sync_synthesis_needed(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
    """Inject/prune synthesis::pending based on review finding drift.
    
    Triggers when open review/concerns findings exist AND the snapshot
    hash differs from the last-synthesized hash.
    """
    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)  # Hash of open review/concerns IDs
    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
    has_synthesis = SYNTHESIS_ID in order

    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash:
        # Need synthesis — inject if not present
        if not has_synthesis:
            order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)  # <-- INJECTED AT FRONT
            result.injected = True
    else:
        # Synthesis up to date or no review findings — prune if present
        if has_synthesis:
            order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
            result.pruned = True
```

**Trigger condition:** Review findings (detector in ("review", "concerns")) changed and the snapshot hash differs.

---

### 7. **Building Synthesis Item Details**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/helpers.py:296-361`

The `build_synthesis_item()` returns `None` unless `SYNTHESIS_ID` is in `plan.queue_order`:

```python
def build_synthesis_item(plan: dict, state: dict) -> dict | None:
    """Build a synthetic T1 queue item for pending epic synthesis.
    
    Returns None if synthesis is not needed (no synthesis::pending in queue).
    """
    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID

    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in order:
        return None  # <-- CRITICAL: Returns None if not in queue_order

    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)

    # ... builds item with detail about review findings, epics, etc.
    return {
        "id": SYNTHESIS_ID,
        "kind": "synthesis_needed",
        "tier": 1,
        "effective_tier": 1,
        ...
    }
```

---

### 8. **Checking Synthesis Pending from Any Command**

There is **no single helper function** yet. You must check manually:

```python
# Option 1: Check the plan queue_order
from desloppify.engine.plan import SYNTHESIS_ID, load_plan

def is_synthesis_pending() -> bool:
    plan = load_plan()
    return SYNTHESIS_ID in plan.get("queue_order", [])

# Option 2: Check after building work queue
from desloppify.engine.work_queue import build_work_queue, QueueBuildOptions

queue = build_work_queue(state, options=QueueBuildOptions(plan=plan))
items = queue.get("items", [])
if items and items[0].get("kind") == "synthesis_needed":
    # Synthesis is pending
```

### 9. **Resolve Command Integration**

**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py`

The `resolve` command (lines 92-134) already has synthesis awareness:
- **Lines 117-128:** When review findings are resolved, it nudges about synthesis staleness
- **Lines 95-130:** After resolve, it checks if synthesis needs re-running

This is a **good model** for where to add gating. But currently, resolve does NOT prevent agents from working if synthesis is pending — it only nudges.

---

### 10. **The Natural Gating Choke Points**

Based on the architecture, these are the places where agents could be **blocked** when synthesis is pending:

#### **Primary Gate (Already Implemented):**
1. **`render_terminal_items()`** (render.py:504-509)
   - When terminal output is shown, synthesis is rendered alone
   - No implementation tasks leak through
   - ✅ **This is the main visual gate**

#### **Secondary Gates (Not Yet Implemented):**
2. **`resolve` command entry** (resolve/cmd.py:58)
   - Could check: "Is synthesis pending? If so, warn/block."
   - Natural place since agents use this to mark findings done

3. **`fix` command entry** (would need to check)
   - Could check: "Is synthesis pending? If so, warn/block."
   - Agents use this to auto-fix code

4. **`show` command** 
   - Could warn when synthesis is pending

5. **`plan done` command** (marking items complete)
   - Could warn/prevent marking non-synthesis items complete while synthesis pending

---

### 11. **Summary: The Simplest Gating Function**

Here's what you'd need to add:

```python
# In desloppify/engine/plan.py or a new gating module

from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID

def is_synthesis_pending() -> bool:
    """Check if synthesis::pending is in the queue."""
    try:
        plan = load_plan()
        return SYNTHESIS_ID in plan.get("queue_order", [])
    except (FileNotFoundError, ValueError):
        return False

def assert_synthesis_not_pending() -> None:
    """Raise if synthesis is pending, preventing agent action."""
    if is_synthesis_pending():
        from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_error
        print_error(
            "Synthesis planning is pending. "
            "Run `desloppify plan synthesize` first, then "
            "run `desloppify next` to see implementation tasks."
        )
        raise SystemExit(1)
```

Then in `resolve/cmd.py`, `fix/*.py`, etc., add:

```python
def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    from desloppify.engine.plan import assert_synthesis_not_pending
    assert_synthesis_not_pending()  # <-- Gate
    # ... rest of command
```

---

### 12. **Key Architectural Insights**

1. **Synthesis is always T1 due to sort key** (-2 sort precedence)
   - It naturally rises to position #1 in any queue

2. **Synthesis acts as a "blocker" in the plan queue**
   - Inserted at front of `queue_order` when triggered
   - Persists until manually marked done

3. **The rendering layer already has the main gate**
   - `render_terminal_items()` prevents info leak
   - Agents won't see implementation tasks unless synthesis is resolved

4. **No current programmatic gate for agents**
   - Agents could theoretically call `resolve` or `fix` while synthesis pending
   - Only visual/UI gate exists (the synthesis card rendering)

5. **Best place to add guards:**
   - `resolve/cmd.py` (agents use this to mark findings done)
   - `fix` command (agents use to auto-fix)
   - Any command that modifies findings while planning is pending

---

## Comprehensive Analysis: Agent-Facing Surfaces for Planning Phase Guardrails

Based on my thorough exploration of the desloppify codebase, here's every surface where agents see output and opportunities to add planning-phase guardrails:

---

### 1. **SKILL.md** (`docs/SKILL.md`)
**Current state:**
- Mentions synthesis in section 2 under "Subjective reviews":
  - "The scan will prompt you when a subjective review is needed — just follow its instructions."
  - Section 3 mentions `plan synthesize` and "Epics"
  - **NO explicit warning** about planning phase vs execution phase
  - Does NOT say "don't execute until synthesis is complete" — only procedurally lists the command

**Issue:** The document treats synthesis as a normal workflow step without emphasizing it as a **gating blocker** for execution.

---

### 2. **`desloppify next` rendering** (`desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`)

**Current gating logic (EXCELLENT):**
- **Lines 502-509:** When `synthesis_needed` is the first item, the code:
  - Renders ONLY the synthesis card
  - Does NOT show any items after it
  - **Early returns** to prevent leaking implementation tasks
  - Displays the planning card with clear steps (1-5) and instructions

**Behavior with `--count N`:**
- If synthesis is first, `--count` is **completely ignored** — only the synthesis card shows
- Agents cannot use `--count 20` to bypass the synthesis blocker
- The gating is **hard** — not just a warning

**Perfect synthesis card** (lines 387-424):
```python
print(colorize("  PLAN — do not proceed until complete", "bold"))
print("  This is a planning step. Do NOT start fixing code until this is done.", "yellow")
```

---

### 3. **`desloppify plan queue`** (`desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py`)

**Current state:**
- Renders a compact table of all queue items
- **Shows synthesis items in the table** (lines 114-120):
  - Displays them with tier, detector, and summary
  - But the table itself provides **no warning**
- **Does NOT gate** — shows subsequent items even if synthesis is pending

**Gap:** An agent could see synthesis in position #1 on the queue and still see items #2, #3, etc., tempting them to skip the planning step.

---

### 4. **`desloppify show`** (`desloppify/app/commands/show/cmd.py`)

**Current state:**
- Renders findings for a specific detector, file, or pattern
- **NO synthesis awareness** — does not check if synthesis is pending
- **NO warning** when showing findings while synthesis is blocked
- Loads and displays `plan` data (lines 303-308) for context but doesn't block or warn

**Gap:** An agent could run `desloppify show review` or `desloppify show <finding-id>` while synthesis is pending and see findings to fix, without any indication they're in planning phase.

---

### 5. **`desloppify fix`** (`desloppify/app/commands/fix/cmd.py`)

**Current state:**
- Simple auto-fix entry point (lines 23-71)
- **NO synthesis check** before running
- **NO warning** that they're in planning phase
- No gating logic at all

**Gap:** An agent could run `desloppify fix unused-imports --dry-run` while synthesis is pending, get a preview of fixes, and be tempted to apply them before completing planning.

---

### 6. **`desloppify resolve`** (`desloppify/app/commands/resolve/cmd.py`)

**Current state:**
- Resolves findings by pattern
- After resolution, nudges about synthesis staleness (lines 116-128):
  ```python
  has_review = any(state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
      for fid in all_resolved)
  if has_review:
      from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHESIS_ID
      if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
          print(colorize("  Tip: review findings changed -- run `desloppify plan synthesize`...", "yellow"))
  ```
- **Reactive only** — nudges AFTER you resolve something, not preventive

**Gap:** No **forward-looking** block or warning to prevent executing when synthesis is pending.

---

### 7. **Scan output / LLM coaching block** (`desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_llm.py`)

**Current state:**
- Lines 304-323: When a living plan exists, prints:
  - `LIVING PLAN ACTIVE: {ordered} ordered, {skipped} skipped.`
  - Focused cluster info
  - Epic summary with strategy
  - "The plan is the single source of truth for work order."
  - "Use `desloppify next` which respects the plan."

**Issue:** 
- **Does NOT mention synthesis** even if `synthesis::pending` is in the queue
- **No explicit warning** when synthesis is pending
- Agents see "Plan is active" but not "Planning is blocked"

---

### 8. **`desloppify plan show`** (`desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py`, lines 18-79)

**Current state:**
- Prints plan metadata: queue count, skipped count, clusters, active focus, epics, strategy
- **No synthesis awareness** — does not special-case or highlight synthesis
- Treats synthesis like any other queue item

**Gap:** Running `desloppify plan show` while synthesis is pending doesn't tell you that synthesis is **blocking**.

---

### 9. **Epic rendering in `next`** (`desloppify/app/commands/next_parts/render.py`, lines 427-466)

**Current state:**
- Renders epic cards with:
  - Thesis, root cause, status
  - **Flags `agent_safe` tag** (line 440):
    ```python
    safe_tag = " agent-safe" if agent_safe else ""
    print(colorize(f"  [Epic — {direction}] ({len(finding_ids)} findings,{safe_tag} dependency_order: {dep_order})", "bold"))
    ```
  
**Issue:**
- Shows if epic is agent-safe or NOT
- **But does NOT block or warn** when epic is not agent-safe
- Agent can still run `desloppify plan focus epic-name` and then `desloppify next` to see the epic's findings

**No guardrail:** A non-agent-safe epic is visible but not blocked.

---

### 10. **`show agent_plan` helper** (`desloppify/app/commands/show/render.py`, lines 205-250)

**Current state:**
- When a living plan is active, shows a compact agent plan summary
- **No synthesis awareness**
- **No warning** about synthesis being pending

## Summary: Opportunities for Planning-Phase Guardrails

| Surface | Current Blocking | Current Warning | Gap |
|---------|------------------|-----------------|-----|
| `desloppify next` | ✅ Hard block on synthesis | ✅ Clear "do not proceed" banner | None (excellent) |
| `desloppify plan queue` | ❌ None | ❌ None | Show synthesis as BLOCKED; warn about it |
| `desloppify show` | ❌ None | ❌ None | Check synthesis pending; add banner |
| `desloppify fix` | ❌ None | ❌ None | Check synthesis pending; warn or block |
| `desloppify resolve` | ❌ None | ❌ Reactive only | Add forward-looking block/warn |
| Scan LLM block | ❌ None | ❌ Omits synthesis status | Add explicit "synthesis pending" line |
| `desloppify plan show` | ❌ None | ❌ None | Highlight synthesis if pending |
| Epic rendering | ⚠️ Partial | ❌ agent_safe flag only | Warn if non-agent-safe epic |

---

## Key Insights

1. **`desloppify next` is nearly perfect** — it already blocks synthesis completely and displays a clear planning card. This is the model to follow.

2. **The main gaps are:**
   - **`show` and `fix` have no checks** — agents can inspect/apply fixes while synthesis is pending
   - **`plan queue` and `plan show` don't highlight synthesis** — the plan's blocking state is invisible in these views
   - **Scan output omits synthesis status** — agents don't see if synthesis is pending after a rescan
   - **Non-agent-safe epics have only a cosmetic flag** — no actual warning or restriction

3. **The synthesis blocker is fragile** — it only works if agents follow the workflow (`desloppify next` → `desloppify show` → `desloppify fix`). They could bypass it by directly querying the state or running other commands.

4. **SKILL.md needs updating** — should explicitly say "Planning phase: run `desloppify plan synthesize` before any execution commands" rather than treating it as a normal procedural step.
